Building on this pipeline, we contribute a high-quality dataset with over 50,000 samples and implement a two-stage training strategy comprising SFT and RL. Specifically, we design step-wise reasoning rewards to ensure logical consistency and intra-group complexity penalty to prevent redundant computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines on X2I, achieving superior generation fidelity among simple-to-complex instructions. The code is released at https://github.com/WeChatCV/Interleaved_Visual_Reasoner.

中文摘要翻译: 最近，统一模型将多模态理解和生成整合到单一框架中。然而，"理解-生成鸿沟"仍然存在：模型虽能捕捉用户意图，但往往无法将这种语义知识转化为精确的像素级操作。这一鸿沟在anything-to-image任务（X2I）中导致两个瓶颈：注意力纠缠瓶颈（盲目规划难以处理复杂提示）和视觉精炼瓶颈（非结构化反馈无法高效纠正缺陷）。本文提出一种新颖框架，使统一模型能够根据指令复杂度和自身能力自主切换生成策略。为此，我们构建了一个分层数据流水线，用于构建三种自适应模式的执行路径：简单情况下的直接生成、质量精炼的自反思，以及分解复杂场景的多步规划。基于该流水线，我们贡献了一个包含5万多个样本的高质量数据集，并实施了包含SFT和RL的两阶段训练策略。具体而言，我们设计了逐步推理奖励以确保逻辑一致性，以及组内复杂度惩罚以防止冗余计算开销。大量实验表明，我们的方法在X2I任务上优于现有基线，在简单到复杂的指令中实现了更高的生成保真度。代码已发布于https://github.com/WeChatCV/Interleaved_Visual_Reasoner。

</details>


### [61] [Vision-Core Guided Contrastive Learning for Balanced Multi-modal Prognosis Prediction of Stroke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14710)
*Liren Chen,Lidong Sun,Mingyan Huang,Junzhe Tang,Yinghui Zhu,Guanjie Wang,Yiqing Xia,Ting Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向缺血性脑卒中预后预测的新型三模态融合模型，利用大语言模型从脑部MRI自动生成半结构化诊断文本，并设计视觉条件双对齐融合模块（VDAFM）实现图像、结构化临床数据与生成文本的深度双向交互，在真实临床数据集上取得了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态方法存在两大局限：一是绝大多数方法仅限于双模态融合，缺乏能够有效整合医学图像、结构化临床数据与非结构化文本这三种模态的统一框架；二是现有方法未能建立模态间的深度双向交互机制，导致融合不充分，从而影响了缺血性脑卒中预后预测的准确性。

Method: 首先，利用大语言模型（LLM）从脑部MRI中自动生成半结构化诊断文本，以此缓解专家标注稀缺问题并提升多模态融合鲁棒性；其次，设计视觉条件双对齐融合模块（VDAFM），以视觉特征作为条件先验引导与生成文本的细粒度交互，并通过双语义对齐损失实现动态深度融合，有效缓解模态异质性。

Result: 在真实世界临床数据集上的大量实验表明，所提模型达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的三模态融合框架通过引入LLM生成文本和VDAFM模块，有效解决了现有方法在模态覆盖和双向交互上的不足，为缺血性脑卒中预后预测提供了更准确、鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Deep learning and multi-modal fusion have demonstrated transformative potential in medical diagnosis by integrating diverse data sources. However, accurate prognosis for ischemic stroke remains challenging due to limitations in existing multi-modal approaches. First, current methods are predominantly confined to dual-modal fusion, lacking a framework that effectively integrates the trifecta of medical images, structured clinical data, and unstructured text. Second, they often fail to establish deep bidirectional interactions between modalities; To address these critical gaps, this paper proposes a novel tri-modal fusion model for ischemic stroke prognosis. Our approach first enriches the data representation by employing a Large Language Model (LLM) to automatically generate semi-structured diagnostic text from brain MRIs. This process not only addresses the scarcity of expert annotations but also serves as a regularized semantic enhancement, improving multimodal fusion robustness. Furthermore, we design a core component termed the Vision-Conditioned Dual Alignment Fusion Module (VDAFM), which strategically uses visual features as a conditional prior to guide fine-grained interaction with the generated text. This module achieves a dynamic and profound fusion through a dual semantic alignment loss, effectively mitigating modal heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on a real-world clinical dataset demonstrate that our model achieves state-of-the-art performance.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习和多模态融合通过整合多样化的数据源，在医学诊断中展现出了变革性的潜力。然而，由于现有多模态方法存在局限，缺血性脑卒中的准确预后仍然具有挑战性。首先，当前方法主要局限于双模态融合，缺乏能够有效整合医学图像、结构化临床数据和非结构化文本这三类模态的框架。其次，这些方法往往无法建立模态间的深度双向交互。为解决这些关键问题，本文提出了一种用于缺血性脑卒中预后预测的新型三模态融合模型。我们的方法首先通过使用大语言模型（LLM）从脑部MRI中自动生成半结构化诊断文本，来丰富数据表示。这一过程不仅缓解了专家标注稀缺的问题，还起到了正则化语义增强的作用，提升了多模态融合的鲁棒性。此外，我们设计了一个核心组件——视觉条件双对齐融合模块（VDAFM），该模块策略性地将视觉特征作为条件先验，以引导与生成文本之间的细粒度交互。该模块通过双语义对齐损失实现了动态且深入的融合，有效缓解了模态异质性。在真实世界临床数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的模型达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [62] [Towards Label-Free Single-Cell Phenotyping Using Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14717)
*Saqib Nazir,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种统一深度学习框架，从无标记差分相衬图像中同时实现白细胞分类和连续蛋白表达回归，采用卷积-Transformer混合架构与跨分支门控模块，并引入LLM生成生物学解释。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无标记单细胞成像虽可替代荧光细胞术，但直接从明场形态推断分子表型仍具挑战，现有方法难以同时实现形态分类与分子表达定量预测。

Method: 采用卷积特征与Transformer全局表征融合的混合架构，通过可学习的跨分支门控模块实现鲁棒形态-分子推断，并引入大语言模型生成生物学总结。

Result: 在BSCCM数据集上，WBC分类准确率达91.3%，CD16表达回归的Pearson相关系数为0.72。

Conclusion: 无标记单细胞成像可用于经济高效的血液学分析，无需荧光染色即可同时实现表型识别与定量生物标志物估计。

Original Abstract: Label-free single-cell imaging offers a scalable, non-invasive alternative to fluorescence-based cytometry, yet inferring molecular phenotypes directly from bright-field morphology remains challenging. We present a unified Deep Learning (DL) framework that jointly performs White Blood Cell (WBC) classification and continuous protein-expression regression from label-free Differential Phase Contrast (DPC) images. Our model employs a Hybrid architecture that fuses convolutional fine-grained texture features with transformer-based global representations through a learnable cross-branch gating module, enabling robust morpho-molecular inference from DPC images. To support downstream interpretability, we further incorporate a Large Language Model (LLM) that generates concise, biologically grounded summaries of the predicted cell states. Experiments on the Berkeley Single Cell Computational Microscopy (BSCCM) and Blood Cells Image benchmarks demonstrate strong performance, achieving a 91.3% WBC classification accuracy and a 0.72 Pearson correlation for CD16 expression regression on BSCCM. These results underscore the promise of label-free single-cell imaging for cost-effective hematological profiling, enabling simultaneous phenotype identification and quantitative biomarker estimation without fluorescent staining. The source code is available at https://github.com/saqibnaziir/Single-Cell-Phenotyping.

中文摘要翻译: 无标记单细胞成像为基于荧光的细胞术提供了一种可扩展、非侵入性的替代方案，但直接从明场形态推断分子表型仍然具有挑战性。我们提出了一个统一的深度学习框架，该框架可从无标记差分相衬图像中联合执行白细胞分类和连续蛋白表达回归。我们的模型采用混合架构，通过可学习的跨分支门控模块融合卷积的细粒度纹理特征与基于Transformer的全局表征，从而实现对DPC图像的鲁棒形态-分子推断。为支持下游可解释性，我们进一步引入大语言模型，生成简洁、具有生物学基础的预测细胞状态总结。在Berkeley单细胞计算显微镜和血细胞图像基准上的实验表明，该方法表现优异，在BSCCM数据集上实现了91.3%的白细胞分类准确率和0.72的CD16表达回归皮尔逊相关系数。这些结果凸显了无标记单细胞成像在经济高效血液学分析中的潜力，可在无需荧光染色的情况下同时实现表型识别与定量生物标志物估计。源代码见https://github.com/saqibnaziir/Single-Cell-Phenotyping。

</details>


### [63] [EARL: Towards a Unified Analysis-Guided Reinforcement Learning Framework for Egocentric Interaction Reasoning and Pixel Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14742)
*Yuejiao Su,Xinshen Zhang,Zhen Ye,Lei Yao,Lap-Pui Chau,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EARL框架，通过两阶段解析和强化学习将粗粒度交互语义转化为细粒度的像素级问答与定位，在自我中心视觉理解任务上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM在自我中心视觉的人-环境交互理解中，难以同时实现准确的交互推理和精细的像素级定位。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段对自我中心交互进行整体解析，生成结构化文本描述；第二阶段利用分析引导特征合成器（AFS）融合全局交互描述符作为语义先验，基于用户查询生成文本答案和像素级掩码。通过多面奖励函数和GRPO优化异构输出。

Result: 在Ego-IRGBench上像素级定位cIoU达65.48%，超越此前基于RL的方法8.37%；在EgoHOS上的OOD定位结果展现了强迁移能力。

Conclusion: EARL通过将全局交互语义显式迁移到查询导向的问答与定位中，有效提升了自我中心视觉的细粒度理解能力，并具备良好的跨场景泛化性。

Original Abstract: Understanding human--environment interactions from egocentric vision is essential for assistive robotics and embodied intelligent agents, yet existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs) still struggle with accurate interaction reasoning and fine-grained pixel grounding. To this end, this paper introduces EARL, an Egocentric Analysis-guided Reinforcement Learning framework that explicitly transfers coarse interaction semantics to query-oriented answering and grounding. Specifically, EARL adopts a two-stage parsing framework including coarse-grained interpretation and fine-grained response. The first stage holistically interprets egocentric interactions and generates a structured textual description. The second stage produces the textual answer and pixel-level mask in response to the user query. To bridge the two stages, we extract a global interaction descriptor as a semantic prior, which is integrated via a novel Analysis-guided Feature Synthesizer (AFS) for query-oriented reasoning. To optimize heterogeneous outputs, including textual answers, bounding boxes, and grounding masks, we design a multi-faceted reward function and train the response stage with GRPO. Experiments on Ego-IRGBench show that EARL achieves 65.48% cIoU for pixel grounding, outperforming previous RL-based methods by 8.37%, while OOD grounding results on EgoHOS indicate strong transferability to unseen egocentric grounding scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 从自我中心视角理解人与环境的交互对于辅助机器人和具身智能体至关重要，然而现有的多模态大语言模型在准确的交互推理和细粒度的像素级定位方面仍存在困难。为此，本文提出了EARL——一个基于自我中心分析引导的强化学习框架，该框架将粗粒度的交互语义显式地迁移到查询导向的问答与定位中。具体而言，EARL采用两阶段解析框架，包括粗粒度解读和细粒度响应。第一阶段整体性地解读自我中心交互，并生成结构化的文本描述。第二阶段根据用户查询生成文本答案和像素级掩码。为桥接这两个阶段，我们提取一个全局交互描述符作为语义先验，并通过一种新颖的分析引导特征合成器将其整合，用于查询导向的推理。为优化异构输出（包括文本答案、边界框和定位掩码），我们设计了一个多面奖励函数，并使用GRPO训练响应阶段。在Ego-IRGBench上的实验表明，EARL在像素级定位上达到65.48%的cIoU，相比此前基于强化学习的方法提升了8.37%；同时在EgoHOS上的分布外定位结果显示出对未见过的自我中心定位场景的强大迁移能力。

</details>


### [64] [BioHuman: Learning Biomechanical Human Representations from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14772)
*Yujun Huo,He Zhang,Chentao Song,Honglin Song,Zongyu Zuo,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于仿真框架从现有动作捕捉数据估计肌肉激活，构建大规模数据集BioHuman10M，并设计端到端模型BioHuman，从单目视频联合预测人体运动与肌肉激活，实现了从视觉观察推断内部生物力学状态。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究难以从视觉观察直接推断人体内部生物力学状态（如肌肉激活），且缺乏带有生物力学标注的大规模数据集，限制了运动分析、康复和损伤风险评估等领域的发展。

Method: 提出基于仿真（simulation-based）的框架，从现有动作捕捉数据集估计肌肉激活，构建包含同步视频、运动和肌肉激活的大规模数据集BioHuman10M；在此基础上设计端到端模型BioHuman，以单目视频为输入，联合预测人体运动与肌肉激活。

Result: 实验表明BioHuman能够准确重建运动学运动与肌肉活动，并在多种不同受试者和运动场景中具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于视频的生物力学理解建立了新基准，为物理驱动的人体建模开辟了新可能。

Original Abstract: Understanding human motion beyond surface kinematics is crucial for motion analysis, rehabilitation, and injury risk assessment. However, progress in this domain is limited by the lack of large-scale datasets with biomechanical annotations, and by existing approaches that cannot directly infer internal biomechanical states from visual observations. In this paper, we introduce a simulation-based framework for estimating muscle activations from existing motion capture datasets, resulting in BioHuman10M, a large-scale dataset with synchronized video, motion, and activations. Building on BioHuman10M, we propose BioHuman, an end-to-end model that takes monocular video as input and jointly predicts human motion and muscle activations, effectively bridging visual observations and internal biomechanical states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BioHuman enables accurate reconstruction of both kinematic motion and muscle activity, and generalizes across diverse subjects and motions. We believe our approach establishes a new benchmark for video-based biomechanical understanding and opens up new possibilities for physically grounded human modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 理解表面运动学之外的人体运动对于运动分析、康复和损伤风险评估至关重要。然而，该领域的进展受到缺乏带有生物力学标注的大规模数据集，以及现有方法无法直接从视觉观测推断内部生物力学状态的限制。本文提出了一种基于仿真的框架，从现有动作捕捉数据集中估计肌肉激活，构建了包含同步视频、运动和肌肉激活的大规模数据集BioHuman10M。基于BioHuman10M，我们提出了BioHuman——一种端到端模型，以单目视频为输入，联合预测人体运动和肌肉激活，有效桥接了视觉观测与内部生物力学状态。大量实验表明，BioHuman能够准确重建运动学运动和肌肉活动，并在多种不同受试者和运动场景中具有良好的泛化能力。我们相信，该方法为基于视频的生物力学理解建立了新基准，并为物理驱动的人体建模开辟了新可能。

</details>


### [65] [MonoPRIO: Adaptive Prior Conditioning for Unified Monocular 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14781)
*Leon Davies,Qinggang Meng,Mohamad Saada,Baihua Li,Simon Sølvsten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonoPRIO提出了一种通过自适应先验条件化来解决单目3D检测中尺寸不稳定性问题的方法，在统一多类别和仅汽车设定下均取得了领先性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单目3D检测中，由于遮挡、截断和尺度-深度模糊，度量尺寸和深度难以从单视角确定，且现有方法在多类别统一设置下尺寸预测不稳定，类别差异和局部可见性导致可能的尺寸模式过于分散。

Method: MonoPRIO在尺寸路径中引入自适应先验条件化：离线构建类别感知的尺寸原型，将每个解码器查询路由到软混合先验，应用不确定性感知的对数空间条件化，并在训练中对匹配的正样本使用聚类对齐先验（CAP）正则化。

Result: 在KITTI测试服务器上，MonoPRIO在统一多类别设定中取得最强结果；在仅汽车设定中，在不使用额外数据的情况下，Easy/Moderate/Hard三个难度级别的3D边界框AP均优于对比方法，且计算量远小于MonoCLUE。消融实验表明路由注入和CAP具有互补增益，在部分遮挡和低数据场景下收益最大。

Conclusion: 自适应先验在图像证据不足以确定度量尺寸时最有效，但非典型几何或极端可见性损失仍可能导致路由先验与真实实例几何不匹配。

Original Abstract: Monocular 3D object detection remains challenging because metric size and depth are underdetermined by single-view evidence, particularly under occlusion, truncation, and projection-induced scale-depth ambiguity. Although recent methods improve depth and geometric reasoning, metric size remains unstable in unified multi-class settings, where class variability and partial visibility broaden plausible size modes. We propose MonoPRIO, a unified monocular 3D detector that targets this bottleneck through adaptive prior conditioning in the size pathway. MonoPRIO constructs class-aware size prototypes offline, routes each decoder query to a soft mixture prior, applies uncertainty-aware log-space conditioning, and uses Cluster-Aligned Prior (CAP) regularisation on matched positives during training. On the official KITTI test server, MonoPRIO achieves the strongest fully reported unified multi-class result among methods reporting complete Car, Pedestrian, and Cyclist metrics. In the car-only setting, it also achieves the strongest 3D bounding-box AP across Easy/Moderate/Hard categories among compared methods without extra data, while using substantially less compute than MonoCLUE. Ablations and diagnostics show complementary gains from routed injection and CAP, with the largest benefits in ambiguity-prone, partially occluded, and low-data regimes. These findings indicate that adaptive priors are most effective when image evidence underdetermines metric size, while atypical geometry or extreme visibility loss can still cause mismatch between routed priors and true instance geometry. Code, trained models, result logs, and reproducibility material are available at https://github.com/bigggs/MonoPRIO.

中文摘要翻译: 单目3D目标检测仍然具有挑战性，因为度量尺寸和深度难以从单视角证据中确定，特别是在遮挡、截断以及投影引起的尺度-深度模糊情况下。尽管近期方法改进了深度和几何推理，但在统一的多类别设置中，度量尺寸仍然不稳定——类别差异和局部可见性拓宽了可能的尺寸模式。我们提出MonoPRIO，一种统一的单目3D检测器，通过在尺寸路径中进行自适应先验条件化来针对这一瓶颈。MonoPRIO离线构建类别感知的尺寸原型，将每个解码器查询路由到软混合先验，应用不确定性感知的对数空间条件化，并在训练过程中对匹配的正样本使用聚类对齐先验（CAP）正则化。在官方KITTI测试服务器上，MonoPRIO在报告完整Car、Pedestrian和Cyclist指标的方法中取得了最强的统一多类别结果。在仅汽车设定下，它还在不使用额外数据的方法中，在Easy/Moderate/Hard类别上取得了最强的3D边界框AP，同时计算量显著低于MonoCLUE。消融实验和诊断分析表明，路由注入和CAP具有互补增益，在易模糊、部分遮挡和低数据场景中收益最大。这些发现表明，当图像证据不足以确定度量尺寸时，自适应先验最为有效，而非典型几何或极端可见性损失仍可能导致路由先验与真实实例几何不匹配。代码、训练好的模型、结果日志和可复现性材料可在https://github.com/bigggs/MonoPRIO获取。

</details>


### [66] [Do Composed Image Retrieval Benchmarks Require Multimodal Composition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14787)
*Matteo Attimonelli,Alessandro De Bellis,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Rohit Saxena,Monica Sekoyan,Wai-Chung Kwan,Claudio Pomo,Alessandro Suglia,Dietmar Jannach,Tommaso Di Noia,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了组合图像检索（CIR）任务中存在单模态捷径（unimodal shortcuts）问题：大量查询（32.2%-83.6%）仅需单模态信息即可解决，导致高性能并非来自真正的多模态组合。通过两阶段审计和人工验证，发现现有基准测试混杂了捷径可解、噪声和真正组合性查询，高估了模型的多模态组合能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 质疑并检验一个广泛假设：CIR基准测试中的高性能是否真正源于多模态组合能力，还是可能来自单模态捷径。

Method: 两阶段审计：（1）通过跨模型分析识别捷径可解查询；（2）对4,741个无捷径查询进行人工验证，筛选出1,689个格式良好的查询并重新评估模型。

Result: 在四个CIR基准测试和11个通用多模态嵌入模型上，32.2%-83.6%的查询可仅凭单模态解决；人工验证后仅1,689个查询为格式良好；重新评估显示模型准确率下降但多模态依赖度提升。

Conclusion: 当前CIR基准测试混淆了捷径可解、噪声和真正组合性查询，导致对模型多模态组合能力的高估；需要在查询层面进行更严格的验证和审计。

Original Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is a multimodal retrieval task where a query consists of a reference image and a textual modification, and the goal is to retrieve a target image satisfying both. In principle, strong performance on CIR benchmarks is assumed to require multimodal composition, i.e., combining complementary information from reference image and textual modification. In this work, we show that this assumption does not always hold. Across four widely used CIR benchmarks and eleven Generalist Multimodal Embedding models, a large fraction of queries can be solved using a single modality (from 32.2% to 83.6%), revealing pervasive unimodal shortcuts. Thus, high CIR performance can arise from unimodal signals rather than true multimodal composition. To better understand this issue, we perform a two-stage audit. First, we identify shortcut-solvable queries through cross-model analysis. Second, we conduct human validation on 4,741 shortcut-free queries, of which only 1,689 are well-formed, with common issues including ambiguous edits and mismatched targets. Re-evaluating models on this validated subset reveals qualitatively different behaviour: queries can no longer be solved with a single modality, and successful retrieval requires combining both inputs. While accuracy decreases, reliance on multimodal information increases. Overall, current CIR benchmarks conflate shortcut-solvable, noisy, and genuinely compositional queries, leading to an overestimation of model capability in multimodal composition.

中文摘要翻译: 组合图像检索（CIR）是一项多模态检索任务，查询由参考图像和文本修改组成，目标是检索满足两者的目标图像。原则上，在CIR基准测试上取得强性能被认为需要多模态组合，即结合参考图像和文本修改中的互补信息。在这项工作中，我们证明这一假设并不总是成立。在四个广泛使用的CIR基准测试和十一个通用多模态嵌入模型上，大量查询（32.2%至83.6%）可以使用单一模态解决，揭示了普遍存在的单模态捷径。因此，高CIR性能可能源于单模态信号而非真正的多模态组合。为了更好理解这一问题，我们进行了两阶段审计。首先，通过跨模型分析识别出捷径可解查询。其次，对4,741个无捷径查询进行人工验证，其中仅有1,689个格式良好，常见问题包括模糊的编辑和不匹配的目标。在该验证子集上重新评估模型揭示了定性不同的行为：查询不再能用单一模态解决，成功检索需要结合两种输入。虽然准确率下降，但对多模态信息的依赖度增加。总体而言，当前的CIR基准测试混淆了捷径可解、噪声和真正组合性的查询，导致对模型多模态组合能力的高估。

</details>


### [67] [COAL: Counterfactual and Observation-Enhanced Alignment Learning for Discriminative Referring Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14795)
*Shukun Jia,Shiyu Hu,Yipei Wang,Ximeng Cheng,Yichao Cao,Xiaobo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COAL框架，通过知识正则化解决指代多目标跟踪（RMOT）中高判别力需求与稀疏语义监督之间的结构性矛盾。该方法利用VLM进行显式语义注入以稠密化观测空间，并结合大语言模型的推理能力进行反事实学习以增强监督，通过层次化多流集成架构将外部知识蒸馏为判别性表示。在Refer-KITTI和Refer-KITTI-V2上取得SOTA，尤其在后者上HOTA提升7.28%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: RMOT面临高判别力需求与稀疏语义监督之间的根本性结构矛盾，尤其在需要细粒度区分的强同质场景中更为突出。在稀疏监督下，模型会过拟合于显著但不充分的线索，导致捷径学习和语义崩溃。

Method: 提出COAL框架，包含三个核心策略：(1) 显式语义注入(ESI)，通过VLM稠密化观测空间并增强实例判别性；(2) 反事实学习(CFL)，利用LLM推理增强监督，执行严格属性验证以实现鲁棒的组合识别；(3) 层次化多流集成(HMSI)架构，将外部知识蒸馏为领域特定的判别性表示。

Result: 在Refer-KITTI和Refer-KITTI-V2基准上验证了COAL的有效性，尤其在极具挑战性的Refer-KITTI-V2上，HOTA指标超越当前最先进方法7.28%。

Conclusion: 知识正则化能够有效解决RMOT中稀疏性与判别性之间的悖论。

Original Abstract: Referring Multi-Object Tracking (RMOT) faces a fundamental structural contradiction between the high-discriminability demand and the sparse semantic supervision. This mismatch is particularly acute in highly homogeneous scenarios that require fine-grained discrimination over complex compositional semantics. However, under sparse supervision, models overfit to salient yet insufficient cues, thereby encouraging shortcut learning and semantic collapse. To resolve this, we propose COAL (Counterfactual and Observation-enhanced Alignment Learning), a framework that advances RMOT beyond isolated structural optimization through knowledge regularization. First, we introduce Explicit Semantic Injection (ESI) via a VLM to densify the observation space and enhance instance discriminability. Second, leveraging LLM reasoning, we propose Counterfactual Learning (CFL) to augment supervision, enforcing strict attribute verification for robust compositional recognition. These strategies are unified within a Hierarchical Multi-Stream Integration (HMSI) architecture, which distills external knowledge into domain-specific discriminative representations. Experiments on Refer-KITTI and Refer-KITTI-V2 benchmarks validate COAL's efficacy. Notably, it surpasses the state-of-the-art by 7.28% HOTA on the highly challenging Refer-KITTI-V2. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of knowledge regularization for resolving the sparsity-discriminability paradox in RMOT.

中文摘要翻译: 指代多目标跟踪（RMOT）面临高判别力需求与稀疏语义监督之间的根本性结构矛盾。这种不匹配在需要细粒度区分复杂组合语义的强同质场景中尤为突出。然而，在稀疏监督下，模型会过拟合于显著但不充分的线索，从而鼓励捷径学习和语义崩溃。为解决这一问题，我们提出COAL（Counterfactual and Observation-enhanced Alignment Learning）框架，该框架通过知识正则化将RMOT推进到超越孤立结构优化的层面。首先，我们通过VLM引入显式语义注入（ESI）以稠密化观测空间并增强实例判别性。其次，利用大语言模型（LLM）推理，我们提出反事实学习（CFL）来增强监督，执行严格的属性验证以实现鲁棒的组合识别。这些策略统一在层次化多流集成（HMSI）架构中，将外部知识蒸馏为领域特定的判别性表示。在Refer-KITTI和Refer-KITTI-V2基准上的实验验证了COAL的有效性。值得注意的是，在极具挑战性的Refer-KITTI-V2上，它超越当前最先进方法7.28%的HOTA指标。这些结果证明了知识正则化在解决RMOT中稀疏性与判别性悖论方面的有效性。

</details>


### [68] [Probing into Camera Control of Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14815)
*Chen Hou,Christian Rupprecht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将视频生成模型中的相机控制重新建模为位移场形式的几何引导，通过可微重采样隐特征实现无需训练、即插即用的高效相机控制，并利用该方法作为探针系统性地研究了多种视频扩散模型的相机控制能力及其内在偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常通过学习从相机运动到视频的隐式映射来实现相机控制，但这类方法依赖额外相机模块和配对数据，受限于数据集规模、多样性和场景动态性，易使模型产生偏置并损害基础模型的先验知识。因此，作者提出一种新的视角：相机控制不应被视为隐式映射问题，而应被当作一种几何引导。

Method: 将相机控制重新表述为一组位移场（displacement fields），并在去噪过程中通过对隐特征进行可微重采样（differentiable resampling）来施加这些位移场，从而在无需训练的情况下实现对视频扩散模型的相机控制。

Result: 该方法在多种质量指标上相比微调基线仅出现最小程度的退化，实现了有效的相机控制；此外，利用该无需训练的方法作为探针，识别了代表性视频模型在相机控制上的共性偏差和差异，并在多视图生成任务上对其性能进行了基准测试。

Conclusion: 相机控制可以通过位移场形式的几何引导来实现，无需训练即可适用于大多数视频扩散模型，并且该方法还能用作探针工具，揭示基础模型在相机控制方面的能力、偏差及其在3D/4D任务中的潜力。

Original Abstract: Video is a rich and scalable source of 3D/4D visual observations, and camera control is a key capability for video generation models to produce geometrically meaningful content. Existing approaches typically learn a mapping from camera motion to video using additional camera modules and paired data. However, such datasets are often limited in scale, diversity, and scene dynamics, which can bias the model toward a narrow output distribution and compromise the strong prior learned by the base model. These limitations motivate a different perspective on camera control. In this paper, we show that camera control need not be modeled as an implicit mapping problem, but can instead be treated as a form of geometric guidance that induces displacements across frames. Specifically, we reformulate camera control into a set of displacement fields and apply them via differentiable resampling of latent features during denoising. Our simple approach achieves effective camera control with minimal degradation across diverse quality metrics compared to fine-tuned baselines. Since our method is applicable to most video diffusion models without training, it can also serve as a probe to study the camera control capabilities of base models. Using this probe, we identify universal biases shared by representative video models, as well as disparities in their responses to camera control. Finally, we benchmark their performance in multi-view generation, offering insights into their potential for 3D/4D tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 视频是丰富且可扩展的3D/4D视觉观测来源，而相机控制是视频生成模型生成具有几何意义内容的关键能力。现有方法通常通过学习从相机运动到视频的隐式映射，借助额外的相机模块和配对数据来实现。然而，此类数据集在规模、多样性和场景动态性方面往往有限，这会使模型偏向狭窄的输出分布，并损害基础模型所学习的强先验知识。这些局限性激发了对相机控制的不同视角。在本文中，我们证明相机控制无需建模为隐式映射问题，而是可以被视为一种诱导帧间位移的几何引导。具体而言，我们将相机控制重新表述为一组位移场，并在去噪过程中通过对隐特征进行可微重采样来施加这些位移场。与微调基线相比，我们简洁的方法在多种质量指标上实现了有效的相机控制，且退化程度极小。由于我们的方法无需训练即可适用于大多数视频扩散模型，它还可以作为探针来研究基础模型的相机控制能力。利用这一探针，我们识别了代表性视频模型所共有的普遍偏差，以及它们在响应相机控制时的差异。最后，我们对其在多视图生成中的性能进行了基准测试，为它们在3D/4D任务中的潜力提供了见解。

</details>


### [69] [The Velocity Deficit: Initial Energy Injection for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14819)
*Linze Li,Zong-Wei Hong,Shen Zhang,Bo Lin,Jinglun Li,Yao Tang,Jiajun Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现流匹配（Flow Matching）在高维实践中存在“速度赤字”问题——MSE目标函数系统性低估速度幅度，导致生成样本无法到达数据流形（即“积分滞后”）。为此提出“初始能量注入”策略，包含两种方法：基于训练的幅度感知流匹配（MAFM）和无训练的尺度调度校正器（SSC）。SSC仅需一行代码且无需重新训练，在ImageNet-1k上将FID从13.68提升至7.58（改进44.6%），实现5倍加速，并泛化到文生图和高分辨率生成任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 流匹配理论保证恒定速度轨迹，但在高维实践中出现速度赤字：MSE损失函数系统性低估速度幅度，导致生成样本因速度不足而无法到达数据流形，即积分滞后现象。

Method: 提出初始能量注入策略，包括两种互补方法：(1) 基于训练的幅度感知流匹配（MAFM）；(2) 无需训练的尺度调度校正器（SSC），仅需一行代码修改。两者均基于一个关键不对称性发现：速度收缩在轨迹起始处导致有害的动能停滞，在轨迹末端却起到有益的去噪作用。

Result: SSC在ImageNet-1k（256x256）上将FID从13.68提升至7.58（改进44.6%），实现5倍加速，50步生成器（FID 7.58）超越250步基线（FID 8.65）。方法泛化到文生图任务，在MS-COCO上FID提高约22%。

Conclusion: 初始能量注入有效解决了高维流匹配中的速度赤字和积分滞后问题，SSC方法以零额外训练成本和极小代码修改实现显著性能提升与加速，并具有良好的任务泛化能力。

Original Abstract: While Flow Matching theoretically guarantees constant-velocity trajectories, we identify a critical breakdown in high-dimensional practice: the Velocity Deficit. We show that the MSE objective systematically underestimates velocity magnitude, causing generated samples to fail to reach the data manifold-a phenomenon we term Integration Lag. To rectify this, we propose Initial Energy Injection, instantiated via two complementary methods: the training-based Magnitude-Aware Flow Matching (MAFM) and the training-free Scale Schedule Corrector (SSC). Both are grounded in our discovery of a crucial asymmetry: velocity contraction causes harmful kinetic stagnation at the trajectory's start, yet acts as a beneficial denoising mechanism at its end. Empirically, SSC yields significant efficiency gains with zero retraining and just one line of code. On ImageNet-1k (256x256), it improves FID by 44.6% (from 13.68 to 7.58) and achieves a 5x speedup, enabling a 50-step generator (FID 7.58) to beat a 250-step baseline (FID 8.65). Furthermore, our methods generalize to Text-to-Image tasks and high-resolution generation, improving FID on MS-COCO by ~22%.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管流匹配（Flow Matching）在理论上保证了恒定速度轨迹，但我们发现了高维实践中一个关键的失效现象：速度赤字（Velocity Deficit）。我们表明，MSE目标函数系统性低估了速度幅度，导致生成样本无法到达数据流形——我们将这一现象称为积分滞后（Integration Lag）。为解决此问题，我们提出了初始能量注入（Initial Energy Injection），通过两种互补方法实现：基于训练的幅度感知流匹配（MAFM）和无训练的尺度调度校正器（SSC）。两者均基于我们发现的一个关键不对称性：速度收缩在轨迹起始处导致有害的动能停滞，但在轨迹末端却起到有益的去噪作用。实验表明，SSC在无需重新训练且仅需一行代码的情况下，实现了显著的效率提升。在ImageNet-1k（256×256）上，它将FID提升了44.6%（从13.68降至7.58），并实现了5倍加速，使50步生成器（FID 7.58）超越了250步基线（FID 8.65）。此外，我们的方法泛化到文生图（Text-to-Image）任务和高分辨率生成，在MS-COCO上将FID提升了约22%。

</details>


### [70] [Editor's Choice: Evaluating Abstract Intent in Image Editing through Atomic Entity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14842)
*Mor Ventura,Roy Hirsch,Yonatan Bitton,Regev Cohen,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文正式定义了抽象图像编辑，提出了基于实体粒度的评估框架Entity-Rubrics和首个抽象图像编辑基准AbstractEdit，发现现有模型在意图保持和内容保留间难以平衡，而整合高级LLM文本编码器和迭代推理能带来显著改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准主要关注显式、字面指令，忽略了人类自然交流中使用的抽象概念（如"情绪"），导致抽象指令编辑这一重要领域未被充分探索。

Method: (1) 形式化定义了抽象图像编辑及其分类体系；(2) 提出Entity-Rubrics框架，将抽象编辑拆解为实体级评估，实现与人类判断的高度相关；(3) 构建AbstractEdit基准数据集，覆盖多样化真实场景。

Result: 在11个领先模型上的评估显示，标准架构在意图保持和内容保留间难以平衡，常出现欠编辑或过编辑问题；整合高级LLM文本编码器和迭代推理是推动实质性改进的关键。

Conclusion: 基于实体的范式可泛化至奖励模型、辅助模型理解抽象交流、以及在测试时批评循环中定位特定失败，为弥合机器执行与人类自然交流间的差距奠定基础。

Original Abstract: Humans naturally communicate through abstract concepts like "mood". However, current image editing benchmarks focus primarily on explicit, literal commands, leaving abstract instructions largely underexplored. In this work, we first formalize the definition and taxonomy of abstract image editing. To measure instruction-following in this challenging domain, we introduce Entity-Rubrics, a framework that breaks down abstract edits into individual, entity-level assessments and achieves strong correlation with human judgment. Alongside this framework, we contribute AbstractEdit, the first benchmark dedicated to abstract image editing across diverse real-world scenes. Evaluating 11 leading models on this dataset reveals a fundamental challenge: standard architectures struggle to balance intent and preservation, commonly defaulting to under-editing or over-editing. Our analysis demonstrates that driving meaningful improvements relies heavily on integrating advanced LLM text encoders and iterative thinking. Looking forward, our entity-based paradigm can generalize beyond assessment to serve as a reward model, enable models to correctly interpret abstract communication, or highlight specific failures in test-time critique loops. Ultimately, we hope this work serves as a stepping stone toward seamless multimodal interaction, closing the gap between rigid machine execution and the natural, open-ended way humans communicate.

中文摘要翻译: 人类自然通过"情绪"等抽象概念进行交流。然而，当前的图像编辑基准主要关注显式、字面指令，抽象指令在很大程度上仍未得到探索。在这项工作中，我们首先正式定义了抽象图像编辑的概念及其分类体系。为了衡量这一具有挑战性领域中的指令遵循能力，我们提出了Entity-Rubrics框架，该框架将抽象编辑分解为逐个实体的评估，并与人类判断实现了强相关性。伴随该框架，我们贡献了AbstractEdit——首个面向多样化真实场景中抽象图像编辑的基准数据集。在该数据集上评估11个领先模型揭示了一个根本性挑战：标准架构难以在意图保持和内容保留之间取得平衡，通常默认出现欠编辑或过度编辑。我们的分析表明，推动有意义的改进在很大程度上依赖于集成先进的大语言模型文本编码器和迭代推理。展望未来，我们的基于实体的范式可以超越评估本身，泛化到作为奖励模型、使模型能够正确解释抽象交流，或在测试时批评循环中突出特定失败。最终，我们希望这项工作能够成为迈向无缝多模态交互的垫脚石，弥合僵化的机器执行与人类自然开放式交流之间的鸿沟。

</details>


### [71] [MechVerse: Evaluating Physical Motion Consistency in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14843)
*Rahul Jain,Mayank Patel,Asim Unmesh,Karthik Ramani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MechVerse，一个用于评估图像到视频生成模型中运动力学一致性的基准，包含21,156个合成视频片段和1,357个机械装配体，实验表明现有模型在视觉保真度上表现良好，但在生成符合运动学约束的运动时存在显著缺陷，且错误率随耦合复杂度增加而上升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的文本和图像条件视频生成模型在视觉保真度和时间连贯性上表现良好，但常常无法生成受运动学和几何约束支配的运动，例如刚体部件需保持刚性、接触/耦合关系以及运动在连接部件间的一致传递。这些约束在机械装配体中尤为明显，生成的视频可能看起来合理但实际上违反了机械运动原理。

Method: 构建了 MechVerse 基准：包含来自141个类别、1,357个机械装配体的21,156个合成视频片段，按运动学复杂度分为三个层级（独立关节运动、成对耦合、多部件密集耦合）。每个视频配有结构化提示描述部件身份、静止支撑、运动部件、运动基元、方向、速度/幅度和部件间依赖关系。使用标准视频指标、指令遵循评分和人工判断进行评估。

Result: 现有模型能够保持外观和时间平滑性，但在生成力学可接受的运动方面存在失败，且错误率随耦合复杂度的增加而上升。

Conclusion: MechVerse 提供了一个用于衡量和改进从图像和语言输入进行机制感知视频生成的基准。

Original Abstract: Text- and image-conditioned video generation models have achieved strong visual fidelity and temporal coherence, but they often fail to generate motion governed by kinematic and geometric constraints. In these settings, object parts must remain rigid, maintain contact or coupling with neighboring components, and transfer motion consistently across connected parts. These requirements are especially explicit in articulated mechanical assemblies, where motion is constrained by rigid-link geometry, contact/coupling relations, and transmission through kinematic chains. A generated video may therefore appear plausible while violating the intended mechanism, such as rotating a part that should translate, deforming a rigid component, breaking coupling between parts, or failing to move downstream components. To evaluate this gap, We introduce MechVerse, a benchmark for mechanically consistent image-to-video generation. MechVerse contains 21,156 synthetic clips from 1,357 mechanical assemblies across 141 categories, organized into three tiers of increasing kinematic complexity: independent articulation, pairwise coupling, and densely coupled multi-part mechanisms. Each clip is paired with a structured prompt describing part identities, stationary supports, moving components, motion primitives, direction, speed/extent, and inter-part dependencies. We evaluate proprietary, open-source, and fine-tuned image-to-video models using standard video metrics, instruction-following scores, and human judgments of motion correctness and kinematic coupling. Results show that current models can preserve appearance and smoothness while failing to generate mechanically admissible motion, with errors increasing as coupling complexity grows. MechVerse provides a benchmark for measuring and improving mechanism-aware video generation from image and language inputs.

中文摘要翻译: 文本和图像条件视频生成模型已经实现了强大的视觉保真度和时间连贯性，但它们通常无法生成受运动学和几何约束支配的运动。在这些设置中，物体部件必须保持刚性，与相邻组件保持接触或耦合关系，并在连接部件间一致地传递运动。这些要求在铰接机械装配体中尤为明确，其中运动受到刚性连杆几何、接触/耦合关系以及通过运动链的传动约束。因此，生成的视频可能看起来合理但实际上违反了预期的机制，例如旋转本应平移的部件、使刚性部件变形、破坏部件间的耦合，或未能带动下游组件运动。为了评估这一差距，我们引入了 MechVerse，一个用于机械一致图像到视频生成的基准。MechVerse 包含来自141个类别、1,357个机械装配体的21,156个合成视频片段，按运动学复杂度递增组织为三个层级：独立关节运动、成对耦合和密集耦合的多部件机构。每个视频配有一个结构化提示，描述部件身份、静止支撑、运动部件、运动基元、方向、速度/幅度和部件间依赖关系。我们使用标准视频指标、指令遵循评分以及人工判断（运动正确性和运动学耦合）评估了专有模型、开源模型和微调模型。结果表明，当前模型能够保持外观和平滑性，但在生成力学可接受的运动方面存在失败，且错误率随耦合复杂度增加而上升。MechVerse 提供了一个用于衡量和改进从图像和语言输入进行机制感知视频生成的基准。

</details>


### [72] [Exploring Vision-Language Models for Online Signature Verification: A Zero-Shot Capability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14845)
*Marta Robledo-Moreno,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Ruben Tolosana,Javier Ortega-Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次探索了视觉-语言模型（VLM）在签名验证任务中的零样本表现，发现其在随机伪造场景下可超越有监督系统，但在熟练伪造场景下性能急剧下降，且链式思维推理会因“合理化陷阱”导致性能退化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在通用视觉推理中表现强劲，但在严谨的生物特征识别任务（如签名验证）中的应用尚未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将原始动态时间序列转换为静态图像（通过笔划透明度编码压力信息），并设计了一种基于潜在token概率提取的评分协议，以零样本方式评估VLM（GPT-5.2和Gemini 2.5 Pro）在SVC基准上的签名验证性能。

Result: 在随机伪造场景中，GPT-5.2在移动端任务上达到0.32%的等错误率，超越有监督SOTA系统；在熟练伪造场景中性能显著下降，且链式思维推理会触发“合理化陷阱”——模型产生运动学幻觉，将伪造伪影误解为自然变异。

Conclusion: VLM在签名验证任务上展现出与信号质量和伪造类型高度相关的性能两极化：在简单场景中具有突破性，但在复杂场景中面临严重限制，CoT推理甚至会加剧性能退化。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in general visual reasoning, yet their applicability to rigorous biometric tasks remains unexplored. This work presents an exploratory study evaluating the zero-shot performance of state-of-the-art VLMs (GPT-5.2 and Gemini 2.5 Pro) on the Signature Verification Challenge (SVC) benchmark. To enable visual processing, raw kinematic time-series are converted into static images, encoding pressure information into stroke opacity whenever available in the source data. Furthermore, we introduce a scoring protocol that extracts latent token probabilities to compute robust biometric scores. Experimental results reveal a significant performance dichotomy dependent on signal quality and forgery type. In random forgery scenarios, the zero-shot VLM achieves exceptional discrimination, with GPT-5.2 reaching an Equal Error Rate of 0.32% in mobile tasks, outperforming supervised state-of-the-art systems. Conversely, in skilled forgery scenarios, where the task is more challenging because both signatures are almost identical, the results are significantly worse, and a critical "Rationalization Trap" emerges: chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning degrades performance as the model produces kinematic hallucinations to justify forgery artifacts as natural variability.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）的最新进展已展现出强大的通用视觉推理能力，但其在严谨的生物特征识别任务中的适用性仍有待探索。本文提出了一项探索性研究，评估了最先进的VLM（GPT-5.2和Gemini 2.5 Pro）在签名验证挑战（SVC）基准上的零样本性能。为实现视觉处理，我们将原始动态时间序列转换为静态图像，并在源数据可用时，将压力信息编码为笔划透明度。此外，我们引入了一种评分协议，通过提取潜在token概率来计算稳健的生物特征分数。实验结果表明，性能存在显著的两极化，取决于信号质量和伪造类型。在随机伪造场景中，零样本VLM实现了卓越的判别能力，GPT-5.2在移动端任务中达到了0.32%的等错误率，超越了有监督的最先进系统。相反，在熟练伪造场景中，由于两个签名几乎相同，任务更具挑战性，结果显著变差，并且出现了一个关键的“合理化陷阱”：链式思维（CoT）推理会降低性能，因为模型会产生运动学幻觉，将伪造伪影解释为自然变异。

</details>


### [73] [SR-Prominence: A Crowdsourced Protocol and Dataset Suite for Perceptually-Weighted Super-Resolution Artifact Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14847)
*Ivan Molodetskikh,Kirill Malyshev,Mark Mirgaleev,Nikita Zagainov,Evgeney Bogatyrev,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“伪影显著性”作为超分图像质量评估新标准，构建SR-Prominence数据集（含3935个已标注显著性的伪影掩膜），发现48.2%的实验室二分类伪影实际不被多数观众察觉，并揭示了经典全参考指标（SSIM、DISTS）在局部显著性评估中优于无参考方法和专用伪影检测器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有超分方法生成的图像虽有视觉细节但常引入伪影，而现有检测方法对所有伪影一视同仁，忽略了它们在感知影响上的巨大差异（有些几乎不可见，有些非常令人不适），因此需要以感知显著性为目标的评估标准。

Method: (1) 定义“伪影显著性”为认为标注区域含明显伪影的观看者比例；(2) 设计众包标注协议构建SR-Prominence数据集（含DeSRA、Open Images、Urban100及无真实参考的Urban100-HR共3935个伪影掩膜）；(3) 重新标注DeSRA发现48.2%的二分类伪影不被多数观众注意到；(4) 系统性审计现有SR伪影检测器、图像质量指标和SR方法。

Result: (1) 经典全参考指标SSIM和DISTS在局部显著性评估上表现意外强；(2) 无参考IQA方法和专用伪影检测器在跨数据集和有无真实参考的场景下泛化失败；(3) 48.2%的DeSRA二分类伪影不被多数观众视为明显伪影。

Conclusion: SR-Prominence数据集及其评估协议将超分伪影评估从二分类缺陷存在性转向感知影响力，并提供无需额外众包的基准测试方案。

Original Abstract: Modern image super-resolution methods generate detailed, visually appealing results, but they often introduce visual artifacts: unnatural patterns and texture distortions that degrade perceived quality. These defects vary widely in perceptual impact--some are barely noticeable, while others are highly disturbing--yet existing detection methods treat them equally. We propose artifact prominence as an evaluative target, defined as the fraction of viewers who judge a highlighted region to contain a noticeable artifact. We design a crowdsourced annotation protocol and construct SR-Prominence, a dataset suite containing 3,935 artifact masks from DeSRA, Open Images, Urban100, and a realistic no-ground-truth Urban100-HR setting, annotated with prominence. Re-annotating DeSRA reveals that 48.2% of its in-lab binary artifacts are not noticed by a majority of viewers. Across the suite, we audit SR artifact detectors, image-quality metrics, and SR methods. We find that classical full-reference metrics, especially SSIM and DISTS, provide surprisingly strong localized prominence signals, whereas no-reference IQA methods and specialized artifact detectors often fail to generalize across datasets and reference settings. SR-Prominence is released with an objective scoring protocol that allows new metrics to be benchmarked on our suite without further crowdsourcing. Together, the data and protocols enable SR artifact evaluation to move from binary defect presence toward perceptual impact. SR-Prominence is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/imolodetskikh/sr-artifact-prominence.

中文摘要翻译: 现代图像超分辨率方法能生成细节丰富、视觉吸引人的结果，但它们常常引入视觉伪影——不自然的图案和纹理失真，从而降低了感知质量。这些缺陷在感知影响上差异巨大——有些几乎不可察觉，而另一些则非常令人不适——然而现有的检测方法对它们一视同仁。我们提出将伪影显著性作为评估目标，定义为认为标注区域含有明显伪影的观看者比例。我们设计了一个众包标注协议，并构建了SR-Prominence数据集套件，包含来自DeSRA、Open Images、Urban100以及一个无真实参考的现实场景Urban100-HR的3935个伪影掩膜，并标注了显著性。重新标注DeSRA发现，其48.2%的实验室二分类伪影实际上不被大多数观看者注意到。在整个数据集套件上，我们对SR伪影检测器、图像质量指标和SR方法进行了审计。我们发现经典的全参考指标，尤其是SSIM和DISTS，在局部显著性信号上表现出令人惊讶的强大能力，而无参考IQA方法和专用伪影检测器往往在跨数据集和有无参考设置下泛化失败。SR-Prominence附带了一个客观评分协议，允许新指标在我们的套件上进行基准测试而无需额外的众包。这些数据和协议共同推动SR伪影评估从二分类缺陷存在性转向感知影响力。SR-Prominence可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/imolodetskikh/sr-artifact-prominence获取。

</details>


### [74] [FactorizedHMR: A Hybrid Framework for Video Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14854)
*Patrick Kwon,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FactorizedHMR提出了一种两阶段框架，将人体网格重建中的确定性躯干-根节点回归与概率流匹配的肢体关节生成相结合，有效处理了人体姿态估计中的非均匀模糊性问题，在遮挡严重和世界空间指标上表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人体网格重建中，不同身体部位的模糊性是不均匀的：躯干姿态和根节点结构通常约束较好，而四肢等远端关节则存在更大的不确定性。现有方法未针对这一非均匀性进行差异化处理。

Method: 提出两阶段框架FactorizedHMR：第一阶段用确定性回归模块恢复稳定的躯干-根节点锚点；第二阶段用概率流匹配模块完成剩余的非躯干关节生成。结合复合目标表示、几何感知监督和特征感知的无分类器引导，在保持躯干-根节点锚点的同时改善模糊关节的单参考恢复。还引入了一个合成数据流水线，提供多视角下配对的图像-相机-运动监督。

Result: 在相机空间和世界空间基准上，FactorizedHMR与强基线方法相比保持竞争力，在遮挡严重场景和漂移敏感的世界空间指标上取得了最明显的提升。

Conclusion: 通过将躯干-根节点与肢体关节分阶段差异化处理，FactorizedHMR有效解决了人体网格重建中非均匀模糊性问题，在遮挡场景下展现出显著优势。

Original Abstract: Human Mesh Recovery (HMR) is fundamentally ambiguous: under occlusion or weak depth cues, multiple 3D bodies can explain the same image evidence. This ambiguity is not uniform across the body, as torso pose and root structure are often relatively well constrained, whereas distal articulations such as the arms and legs are more uncertain. Building on this observation, we propose FactorizedHMR, a two-stage framework that treats these two regimes differently. A deterministic regression module first recovers a stable torso-root anchor, and a probabilistic flow-matching module then completes the remaining non-torso articulation. To make this completion reliable, we combine a composite target representation with geometry-aware supervision and feature-aware classifier-free guidance, preserving the torso-root anchor while improving single-reference recovery of ambiguity-prone articulation. We also introduce a synthetic data pipeline that provides the paired image-camera-motion supervision under diverse viewpoints. Across camera-space and world-space benchmarks, FactorizedHMR remains competitive with strong baselines, with the clearest gains in occlusion-heavy recovery and drift-sensitive world-space metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 人体网格重建（HMR）本质上存在模糊性：在遮挡或弱深度线索下，多个3D人体可以解释同一图像证据。这种模糊性在身体各部位并不均匀——躯干姿态和根节点结构通常约束较好，而手臂和腿等远端关节则更加不确定。基于这一观察，我们提出FactorizedHMR，一个将这两种情况区别对待的两阶段框架。确定性回归模块首先恢复稳定的躯干-根节点锚点，概率流匹配模块随后完成剩余的非躯干关节生成。为了使这一补全过程可靠，我们结合了复合目标表示、几何感知监督和特征感知的无分类器引导，在保持躯干-根节点锚点的同时改善模糊关节的单参考恢复。我们还引入了一个合成数据流水线，提供多视角下配对的图像-相机-运动监督。在相机空间和世界空间基准上，FactorizedHMR与强基线方法保持竞争力，在遮挡严重场景和漂移敏感的世界空间指标上取得了最明显的提升。

</details>


### [75] [LPH-VTON: Resolving the Structure-Texture Dilemma of Virtual Try-On via Latent Process Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14874)
*Yixin Liu,Baihong Qian,Jinglin Jiang,Jeffery Wu,Yan Chen,Wei Wang,Yida Wang,Lanqing Yang,Guangtao Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LPH-VTON框架，通过时间架构解耦策略，在单一连续去噪过程中先后利用结构偏置模型和纹理偏置模型，解决了虚拟试穿中结构完整性与纹理保真度之间的权衡难题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散方法在虚拟试穿中面临结构完整性与纹理保真度的根本性权衡：强空间约束模型利于几何对齐但抑制纹理，无约束生成先验模型细节丰富但易结构漂移。本文旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出LPH-VTON协同框架，在单一连续去噪过程中进行时间维度的架构解耦：早期阶段使用结构偏置模型建立几何一致的潜在空间支架，后期阶段切换至纹理偏置模型进行高保真细节渲染。

Result: 在VITON-HD标准数据集上，模型实现了帕累托最优平衡，在感知忠实度上达到新基准，同时保持高度竞争力的结构对齐效果。

Conclusion: 通过时间架构解耦策略，在单一去噪过程中融合结构偏置和纹理偏置模型各自的优势，证明了该范式在解决虚拟试穿核心权衡问题上的有效性。

Original Abstract: Virtual Try-On (VTON) aims to synthesize photorealistic images of garments precisely aligned with a person's body and pose. Current diffusion-based methods, however, face a fundamental trade-off between structural integrity and textural fidelity. In this paper, we formalize this challenge as a consequence of complementary inductive biases inherent in prevailing architectures: models heavily reliant on spatial constraints naturally favor geometric alignment but often suppress textures, whereas models dominated by unconstrained generative priors excel at vibrant detail rendering but are prone to structural drift. Based on this diagnosis, we propose LPH-VTON, a new synergistic framework that resolves this tension within a single, continuous denoising process. LPH-VTON strategically decomposes the generation, leveraging a structure-biased model to establish a geometrically consistent latent scaffold in the early stages, before handing over control to a texture-biased model for high-fidelity detail rendering. Extensive experiments validate our approach. Our model achieves a superior Pareto-optimal balance, establishing new benchmarks in perceptual faithfulness while maintaining highly competitive structural alignment across the standard dataset VITON-HD, proving the efficacy of temporal architectural decoupling.

中文摘要翻译: 虚拟试穿（VTON）旨在合成与人体姿态和体型精确对齐的逼真服装图像。然而，当前的扩散方法面临着结构完整性与纹理保真度之间的根本性权衡。本文将该挑战形式化为现有架构中互补性归纳偏置的后果：严重依赖空间约束的模型自然利于几何对齐但往往抑制纹理，而受无约束生成先验主导的模型擅长生动细节渲染但容易发生结构漂移。基于这一诊断，我们提出LPH-VTON，一种新颖的协同框架，在单一连续的降噪过程中解决这一矛盾。LPH-VTON策略性地分解生成过程：早期阶段利用结构偏置模型建立几何一致的潜在空间支架，随后将控制权移交给纹理偏置模型以进行高保真细节渲染。大量实验验证了我们的方法。我们的模型实现了优越的帕累托最优平衡，在标准数据集VITON-HD上建立了感知忠实度的新基准，同时保持高度竞争力的结构对齐，证明了时间架构解耦的有效性。

</details>


### [76] [HeatKV: Head-tuned KV-cache Compression for Visual Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14877)
*Jonathan Cederlund,Axel Berg,Durmus Alp Emre Acar,Chuteng Zhou,Pontus Giselsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeatKV提出了一种针对视觉自回归（VAR）模型的高效KV-cache压缩方法，通过按头分配缓存并根据注意力分数排序实现静态剪枝，在Infinity-2B模型上以2倍压缩比超越现有方法，同时保持或提升图像质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: VAR模型在图像生成中虽质量优异、延迟低，但KV-cache内存消耗极大（每张图像需数GB），严重限制了其实际部署。

Method: 利用小型离线校准集，根据每个注意力头对先前尺度的注意力分数进行排序，据此构建特定内存预算下的静态剪枝调度，实现细粒度的按头差异化缓存分配。

Result: 在Infinity-2B模型上，HeatKV实现了2倍于现有方法的KV-cache压缩比，同时保持或提升了图像保真度、提示对齐度和人类感知评分，达到了VAR模型KV-cache压缩的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 细粒度的按头缓存分配策略能够有效压缩VAR模型的KV-cache，验证了基于注意力分数排序的静态剪枝在内存效率与生成质量之间取得平衡的可行性。

Original Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (VAR) models have recently demonstrated impressive image generation quality while maintaining low latency. However, they suffer from severe KV-cache memory constraints, often requiring gigabytes of memory per generated image. We introduce HeatKV, a novel compression method that adapts cache allocation in each head based on its attention to previously generated scales. Using a small offline calibration set, the attention heads are ranked according to their attention scores over prior scales. Based on this ranking, we construct a static pruning schedule tailored to a given memory budget. Applied to the Infinity-2B model, HeatKV achieves $2 \times$ higher compression ratio in memory allocation for KV cache compared to existing methods, while maintaining similar or better image fidelity, prompt alignment and human perception score. Our method achieves a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) for VAR model KV-cache compression, showcasing the effectiveness of fine-grained, head-specific cache allocation.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉自回归（VAR）模型近期在保持低延迟的同时展示了令人印象深刻的图像生成质量。然而，它们面临着严重的KV-cache内存约束，每生成一张图像往往需要数GB的内存。我们提出HeatKV，一种新颖的压缩方法，该方法根据每个注意力头对先前生成尺度的注意力来调整其缓存分配。通过使用一个小型离线校准集，注意力头根据其对先前尺度的注意力得分进行排序。基于这一排序，我们构建了一个针对给定内存预算量身定制的静态剪枝调度。应用于Infinity-2B模型，HeatKV在KV-cache内存分配中实现了比现有方法高2倍的压缩比，同时保持或更好的图像保真度、提示对齐度和人类感知评分。我们的方法在VAR模型KV-cache压缩方面达到了新的最先进水平，展示了细粒度、按头特定缓存分配的有效性。

</details>


### [77] [Denoising-GS: Gaussian Splatting with Spatial-aware Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14880)
*Qingyuan Zhou,Xinyi Liu,Weidong Yang,Ning Wang,Shuquan Ye,Ben Fei,Ying He,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Denoising-GS，将3DGS优化视为高斯原语去噪过程，通过空间感知去噪框架（空间梯度去噪、不确定性去噪、空间一致性细化）同步优化原语位置与空间结构，在三个基准数据集上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DGS优化方法仅调整原语位置，忽略了底层空间结构，导致因SfM点云稀疏和不完整引入噪声原语。

Method: (1) 设计保持空间优化流的优化器，实现相干定向去噪；(2) 空间梯度去噪策略联合考虑原语空间支撑，实现梯度一致更新；(3) 不确定性去噪模块估计原语级不确定性以剪枝冗余/噪声原语；(4) 空间一致性细化策略在稀疏区域选择性分裂原语以保持结构完整性。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，Denoising-GS一致提升了NVS保真度并保持表示紧凑性，在所有基准上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 将3DGS优化建模为原语去噪过程，通过空间感知框架联合优化位置与空间结构，能有效消除噪声原语并提升新视角合成质量。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have achieved remarkable success in high-fidelity Novel View Synthesis (NVS), yet the optimization process inevitably introduces noisy Gaussian primitives due to the sparse and incomplete initialization from Structure-from-Motion (SfM) point clouds. Most existing methods focus solely on adjusting the positions of primitives during optimization, while neglecting the underlying spatial structure. To this end, we introduce a new perspective by formulating the optimization of 3DGS as a primitive denoising process and propose Denoising-GS, a spatial-aware denoising framework for Gaussian primitives by taking both the positions and spatial structure into consideration. Specifically, we design an optimizer that preserves the spatial optimization flow of primitives, facilitating coherent and directed denoising rather than random perturbations. Building upon this, the Spatial Gradient-based Denoising strategy jointly considers the spatial supports of primitives to ensure gradient-consistent updates. Furthermore, the Uncertainty-based Denoising module estimates primitive-wise uncertainty to prune redundant or noisy primitives, while the Spatial Coherence Refinement strategy selectively splits primitives in sparse regions to maintain structural completeness. Experiments conducted on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that Denoising-GS consistently enhances NVS fidelity while maintaining representation compactness, achieving state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks. Source code and models will be made publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在高保真新视角合成（NVS）方面取得了显著成功，然而由于运动恢复结构（SfM）点云的稀疏性和不完整性，优化过程中不可避免地引入了噪声高斯原语。现有方法大多仅关注优化过程中调整原语的位置，而忽略了潜在的空间结构。为此，我们引入了一个新视角，将3DGS的优化表述为原语去噪过程，并提出Denoising-GS——一种通过同时考虑位置和空间结构来实现高斯原语空间感知去噪的框架。具体而言，我们设计了一个优化器，用于保留原语的空间优化流，从而促进相干且定向的去噪，而非随机扰动。在此基础上，基于空间梯度的去噪策略联合考虑原语的空间支撑，以确保梯度一致的更新。此外，基于不确定性的去噪模块通过估计原语级不确定性来剪枝冗余或噪声原语，而空间一致性细化策略则选择性地在稀疏区域分裂原语以维持结构完整性。在三个基准数据集上进行的实验表明，Denoising-GS在保持表示紧凑性的同时一致地提升了NVS保真度，在所有基准测试中均达到了最先进的性能。源代码和模型将公开提供。

</details>


### [78] [Masked Next-Scale Prediction for Self-supervised Scene Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14885)
*Zhuohao Chen,Zeng Li,Yifei Zhang,Chang Liu,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MNSP（掩蔽下一尺度预测）框架，通过联合跨尺度预测和掩蔽图像重建，显式建模场景文本的分层结构演化，在多个基准数据集上取得最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自监督方法（如MIM）仅在单尺度上操作，无法捕捉场景文本从粗粒度布局到细粒度笔画的分层结构演化特性。

Method: 提出MNSP框架，包含两个核心组件：1）Next-Scale Prediction (NSP)从低分辨率上下文预测高分辨率特征，学习层级表示；2）联合掩蔽图像重建提供局部约束，引导注意力聚焦于文本区域。此外引入多尺度语言对齐模块维护不同分辨率间的语义一致性。

Result: 在Union14M基准上达到86.2%平均准确率，在六个标准数据集上达到96.7%，均达最先进水平。在极端尺度和布局变化下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模跨尺度结构演化，MNSP有效解决了场景文本识别中的层级表示学习问题，为自监督场景文本识别提供了新的有效范式。

Original Abstract: Scene Text Recognition requires modeling visual structures that evolve from coarse layouts to fine-grained character strokes. Training such models relies on large amounts of annotated data. Recent self-supervised approaches, such as Masked Image Modeling (MIM), alleviate this dependency by leveraging large-scale unlabeled data. Yet most existing MIM methods operate at a single spatial scale and fail to capture the hierarchical nature of scene text. In this work, we introduce Masked Next-Scale Prediction (MNSP), a unified self-supervised framework designed to explicitly model cross-scale structural evolution. The framework incorporates Next-Scale Prediction (NSP), which learns hierarchical representations by predicting higher-resolution features from lower-resolution contexts. Naive scale prediction, however, tends to produce spatially diffuse attention, directing the model toward background regions rather than textual structures. MNSP resolves this limitation by jointly learning cross-scale prediction and masked image reconstruction. NSP captures global layout priors across resolutions, while masked reconstruction imposes strong local constraints that guide attention toward informative text regions. A Multi-scale Linguistic Alignment module further maintains semantic consistency across different resolutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MNSP achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 86.2\% average accuracy on the challenging Union14M benchmark and 96.7\% across six standard datasets. Additional analyses show that our method improves robustness under extreme scale and layout variations. Code is available at https://github.com/CzhczhcHczh/MNSP

中文摘要翻译: 场景文本识别需要对从粗粒度布局到细粒度笔画演变的视觉结构进行建模。训练此类模型依赖于大量标注数据。近年来，掩蔽图像建模（MIM）等自监督方法通过利用大规模无标注数据缓解了这一依赖。然而，现有大多数MIM方法仅在单一空间尺度上操作，无法捕获场景文本的层级特性。在这项工作中，我们提出了掩蔽下一尺度预测（MNSP），一种统一的、旨在显式建模跨尺度结构演化的自监督框架。该框架包含下一尺度预测（NSP），通过从低分辨率上下文中预测高分辨率特征来学习层级表示。然而，朴素的尺度预测容易产生空间弥散的注意力，将模型引向背景区域而非文本结构。MNSP通过联合学习跨尺度预测和掩蔽图像重建解决了这一局限：NSP捕获跨分辨率的全局布局先验，而掩蔽重建施加强大的局部约束，引导注意力聚焦于信息性文本区域。多尺度语言对齐模块进一步维护了不同分辨率之间的语义一致性。大量实验表明，MNSP取得了最先进的性能，在具有挑战性的Union14M基准上达到86.2%的平均准确率，在六个标准数据集上达到96.7%。进一步分析显示，我们的方法在极端尺度和布局变化下具有更强的鲁棒性。代码已开源：https://github.com/CzhczhcHczh/MNSP

</details>


### [79] [Your CLIP has 164 dimensions of noise: Exploring the embeddings covariance eigenspectrum of contrastively pretrained vision-language transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14893)
*Jakub Grzywaczewski,Dawid Płudowski,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过谱分解方法发现VLM共享潜在空间中存在大量非语义噪声，这些噪声在数据子集间保持强子群不变性，去除这些噪声维度不仅无害甚至能提升下游任务性能，表明VLM的潜在几何结构主要由架构级噪声而非语义信号主导。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对比预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）的共享潜在空间存在结构异常，并包含非语义的多模态噪声，阻碍了语义信号的纯净表达。

Method: 利用协方差矩阵的谱分解，将VLM的潜在空间分解为多模态语义信号分量和共享噪声子空间。

Result: 噪声几何结构在不同数据子集间表现出强子群不变性；去除这些共享噪声维度对下游任务性能无害，甚至能提升性能。

Conclusion: VLM潜在几何结构中很大一部分是由共享的架构级噪声而非任务相关语义所主导，分离真实语义信号与人为噪声有助于理解现代VLM的表征结构。

Original Abstract: Contrastively pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) serve as powerful feature extractors. Yet, their shared latent spaces are prone to structural anomalies and act as repositories for non-semantic, multi-modal noise. To address this phenomenon, we employ spectral decomposition of covariance matrices to decompose the VLM latent space into a multi-modal semantic signal component and a shared noise subspace. We observe that this noise geometry exhibits strong subgroup invariance across distinct data subsets. Crucially, pruning these shared noise dimensions is mainly harmless, preserving or actively improving downstream task performance. By isolating true semantic signals from artifactual noise, this work provides new mechanistic insights into the representational structure of modern VLMs, suggesting that a substantial fraction of their latent geometry is governed by shared, architecture-level noise rather than task-relevant semantics alone.

中文摘要翻译: 对比预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）可作为强大的特征提取器。然而，它们的共享潜在空间容易产生结构异常，并充当非语义的多模态噪声的存储库。为解决这一现象，我们采用协方差矩阵的谱分解，将VLM潜在空间分解为多模态语义信号分量和共享噪声子空间。我们观察到，这种噪声几何结构在不同数据子集间表现出强子群不变性。关键在于，去除这些共享噪声维度主要是无害的，能够保持甚至主动提升下游任务性能。通过从人为噪声中分离出真实的语义信号，本工作为现代VLM的表征结构提供了新的机制性见解，表明其潜在几何结构的很大一部分是由共享的架构级噪声而非仅由任务相关语义所主导。

</details>


### [80] [SEDiT: Mask-Free Video Subtitle Erasure via One-step Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14894)
*Zheng Hui,Yunlong Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEDiT 提出了一种基于单步扩散Transformer的掩码无关视频字幕擦除方法，通过单阶段框架、理论保证的单步去噪和混合训练策略，实现高效、长时间一致的高清视频字幕擦除。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频字幕擦除方法通常依赖两阶段处理（先分割掩码再修复），掩码精度直接影响完成质量，且两阶段流程存在次优性。因此，作者希望设计一种无需掩码的单阶段端到端框架，直接擦除目标字幕。

Method: (1) 提出掩码无关（mask-free）推理方法，直接擦除字幕；(2) 采用单步去噪生成，基于整流流（rectified flow）理论证明在局部编辑任务下条件最优传输映射和速度场是Lipschitz连续的，保证单步采样可行性；(3) 混合训练策略：偶尔以干净首帧潜变量为条件，增强时序一致性；(4) 分块流式推理，直接处理原始视频避免裁剪拼接带来的可见接缝。

Result: 实验验证了单步去噪的可靠性，并提供了形式化理论证明。方法能够高效处理原生1440p无限长度视频，在时序一致性和擦除质量上表现优异。

Conclusion: SEDiT通过单阶段掩码无关框架、单步扩散和理论保证，实现了高效、高质量、长时间一致的无限制视频字幕擦除，克服了现有两阶段方法的局限。

Original Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in video diffusion models have significantly accelerated the development of video editing techniques. However, existing methods often rely on inpainting video frames based on masked input, which requires extracting the target video mask in advance, and the precision of the segmentation directly affects the quality of the completion. In this paper, we present SEDiT, a novel one-stage video Subtitle Erasure approach via One-step Diffusion Transformer. We introduce a mask-free inference approach that enables direct erasure of the targeted subtitle. The proposed one-stage framework mitigates the sub-optimality inherent in the two-stage processing of prior models. Since subtitle removal is a localized editing task in which most pixels remain unchanged, the underlying distribution shift is minimal, making it well-suited to one-step generation under rectified flow. We empirically validate the reliability of one-step denoising and further provide a formal theoretical justification. Under the localized-editing structure of subtitle removal, the conditional optimal transport (OT) map and its induced rectified flow velocity field are Lipschitz continuous with respect to the latent variable, which underpins the theoretical feasibility of one-step sampling. To address the challenge of long-term temporal consistency, we adopt a hybrid training strategy by occasionally conditioning the model with a clean first-frame latent. This facilitates temporal continuity, allowing each segment during inference to leverage the output of its predecessor. To avoid visible seams caused by cropping and reinserting processed targets, particularly in scenarios involving substantial motion, we feed the original video directly into SEDiT. Thanks to one-step and chunk-wise streaming inference, our method can efficiently handle native 1440p video with infinite length.

中文摘要翻译: 视频扩散模型的最新突破极大地加速了视频编辑技术的发展。然而，现有方法通常依赖于基于掩码输入的视频帧修复，这需要预先提取目标视频掩码，且分割精度直接影响完成质量。在本文中，我们提出了SEDiT，一种新颖的基于单步扩散Transformer的单阶段视频字幕擦除方法。我们引入了一种无需掩码的推理方法，能够直接擦除目标字幕。所提出的单阶段框架缓解了先前模型两阶段处理中固有的次优性问题。由于字幕移除是一种局部编辑任务，其中大多数像素保持不变，潜在的分布偏移极小，因此非常适合在整流流下进行单步生成。我们通过实验验证了单步去噪的可靠性，并进一步提供了形式化的理论证明。在字幕移除的局部编辑结构下，条件最优传输映射及其诱导的整流流速度场关于潜变量是Lipschitz连续的，这为单步采样的理论可行性奠定了基础。为了解决长期时序一致性的挑战，我们采用了一种混合训练策略，偶尔以干净的首帧潜变量为条件对模型进行条件约束。这促进了时序连续性，使得推理过程中每个片段能够利用其前驱的输出。为了避免裁剪和重新插入已处理目标所导致的可见接缝（尤其在涉及大幅度运动的情况下），我们将原始视频直接输入SEDiT。得益于单步和分块流式推理，我们的方法能够高效处理原生1440p无限长度的视频。

</details>


### [81] [SteerSeg: Attention Steering for Reasoning Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14908)
*Ali Cheraghian,Hamidreza Dastmalchi,Abdelwahed Khamis,Morteza Saberi,Aijun An,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SteerSeg 提出通过输入级条件（可学习软提示+思维链提示）引导注意力分布，在不微调大视觉语言模型和分割模型的情况下，显著提升视频推理分割的空间定位能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法利用冻结LVLM的注意力图作为空间先验进行分割，但注意力图针对文本生成优化而非空间定位，导致定位信号弥散、模糊。作者识别出注意力错位是这一范式的关键瓶颈。

Method: (1) 可学习软提示：重塑注意力分布，产生更集中的注意力图；(2) 推理引导的Chain-of-Thought提示：通过CoT推导的属性引导注意力指向正确实例；(3) 将优化后的注意力图转换为点提示，结合相关性评分对候选轨迹进行排序选择，引导分割模型。

Result: 仅在Ref-YouTube-VOS上训练，即可在多个基准上取得良好泛化，显著提升LVLM的空间定位能力。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级的输入级条件控制（软提示+CoT），可以在冻结模型参数的前提下有效解决注意力错位问题，增强LVLM的空间定位能力，兼具高效性与泛化性。

Original Abstract: Video reasoning segmentation requires localizing objects across video frames from natural language expressions, often involving spatial reasoning and implicit references. Recent approaches leverage frozen large vision-language models (LVLMs) by extracting attention maps and using them as spatial priors for segmentation, enabling training-free grounding. However, these attention maps are optimized for text generation rather than spatial localization, often resulting in diffuse and ambiguous grounding signals. In this work, we introduce SteerSeg, a lightweight framework that identifies attention misalignment as the key bottleneck in attention-based grounding and proposes to steer attention at its source through input-level conditioning. SteerSeg combines learnable soft prompts with reasoning-guided Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting. The soft prompts reshape the attention distribution to produce more spatially concentrated maps, while CoT-derived attributes resolve ambiguity among similar objects by guiding attention toward the correct instance. The resulting attention maps are converted into point prompts across keyframes to guide a segmentation model, while candidate tracklets are ranked and selected using correlation-based scoring. Our approach freezes the LVLM and segmentation model parameters and learns only a small set of soft prompts, preserving the model's pretrained reasoning capabilities while significantly improving grounding. Despite being trained only on Ref-YouTube-VOS, SteerSeg generalizes well across diverse benchmarks, significantly improving the spatial grounding capability of LVLMs. Project page: https://steerseg.github.io

中文摘要翻译: 视频推理分割需要根据自然语言表达在视频帧中定位目标对象，这通常涉及空间推理和隐含指代。近期方法利用冻结的大型视觉语言模型（LVLM），通过提取注意力图并将其作为分割的空间先验，实现了无需训练的定位。然而，这些注意力图是针对文本生成而非空间定位优化的，往往产生弥散且模糊的定位信号。本文提出SteerSeg，一个轻量级框架，将注意力错位识别为基于注意力定位的关键瓶颈，并通过输入级条件在其源头引导注意力。SteerSeg结合了可学习软提示与推理引导的思维链（CoT）提示。软提示重塑注意力分布以产生更集中的空间图，而CoT推导的属性通过引导注意力指向正确实例，消解了相似目标间的歧义。最终得到的注意力图被转换为跨关键帧的点提示，用于引导分割模型，同时基于相关性评分对候选轨迹进行排序与选择。我们的方法冻结了LVLM和分割模型的参数，仅学习少量软提示，在保留模型预训练推理能力的同时显著提升了定位效果。尽管仅在Ref-YouTube-VOS上训练，SteerSeg在多种基准上均展现出良好的泛化性，显著增强了LVLM的空间定位能力。项目主页：https://steerseg.github.io

</details>


### [82] [SceneParser: Hierarchical Scene Parsing for Visual Semantics Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14923)
*Pengxin Xu,Xincheng Lin,Luping Xiao,Qing Jiang,Meishan Zhang,Hao Fei,Shanghang Zhang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出层级场景解析（Hierarchical Scene Parsing）这一面向交互的场景理解任务，构建了SceneParser模型和SceneParser-Bench基准，通过结构补全伪标签和课程学习训练VLM实现场景→物体→部件→功能层级解析，实验表明该方法优于现有MLLM和感知拼接管道。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有场景感知研究（如开放词汇定位、部件定位、功能预测等）往往是孤立的预测任务，仅能定位物体、部件或交互点，缺乏面向交互场景理解所需的结构化依赖关系。本文旨在弥合这一差距，提出层级场景解析任务，捕捉场景中物体、部件与功能之间的跨层级绑定关系。

Method: 1) 定义层级场景解析任务，将物理场景表示为场景→物体→部件→功能的显式层级结构，并包含跨层级绑定。2) 提出SceneParser，基于VLM的解析器，采用统一层级生成方式训练，结合结构补全伪标签（structural-completion pseudo labels）和课程学习策略。3) 构建SceneParser-Bench大规模基准，包含11万训练图像、5000验证图像、77.7万物体、114万部件、174万功能标注及174万有效物体-部件-功能链实例。4) 引入Level-1到Level-3条件度量指标和ParseRate，评估定位、跨层级绑定和层级完整性。

Result: 1) 现有MLLM和感知拼接管道在SceneParser-Bench上难以处理层级解析任务。2) SceneParser在结构化感知性能上显著更强。3) 消融实验、在COCO和AGD20K上的评估以及下游规划探测实验表明SceneParser与常规任务兼容，并为视觉理解提供可操作的表征。

Conclusion: 层级场景解析作为一种新的交互导向场景理解范式是有效的，SceneParser通过结构补全伪标签和课程学习在层级解析上取得优越表现，且与现有常规任务兼容，可为视觉理解提供可操作的层级表征。

Original Abstract: General scene perception has progressed from object recognition toward open-vocabulary grounding, part localization, and affordance prediction. Yet these capabilities are often realized as isolated predictions that localize objects, parts, or interaction points without capturing the structured dependencies needed for interaction-oriented scene understanding. To address this gap, we introduce Hierarchical Scene Parsing, an interaction-oriented parsing task that represents physical scenes as explicit scene -> object -> part -> affordance hierarchies with cross-level bindings. We instantiate this task with SceneParser, a VLM-based parser trained for unified hierarchical generation with structural-completion pseudo labels and curriculum learning. To support training and evaluation, we construct SceneParser-Bench, a large-scale benchmark built with a scalable hierarchical data engine, containing 110K training images, a 5K validation split, 777K objects, 1.14M parts, 1.74M affordance annotations, and 1.74M valid object-part-affordance chain instances. We further introduce Level-1 to Level-3 conditional metrics and ParseRate to evaluate localization, cross-level binding, and hierarchical completeness. Experiments show that existing MLLMs and perception-stitching pipelines struggle with hierarchical parsing on our SceneParser-Bench, while SceneParser achieves stronger structure-aware performance. Besides, ablations, evaluations on COCO and AGD20K, and a downstream planning probe demonstrate that our SceneParser is compatible with conventional tasks and provides an actionable representation for visual understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 通用场景感知已从物体识别发展到开放词汇定位、部件定位和功能预测。然而，这些能力通常以孤立的预测形式实现，仅能定位物体、部件或交互点，未能捕捉交互导向场景理解所需的结构化依赖关系。为弥补这一不足，我们提出层级场景解析（Hierarchical Scene Parsing），一种面向交互的解析任务，将物理场景表示为显式的场景→物体→部件→功能层级结构，并包含跨层级绑定。我们通过SceneParser实例化该任务，这是一个基于VLM的解析器，采用统一层级生成方式训练，结合结构补全伪标签和课程学习策略。为支持训练与评估，我们构建了SceneParser-Bench，一个大规模基准数据集，借助可扩展的层级数据引擎构建，包含11万张训练图像、5000张验证集、77.7万个物体、114万个部件、174万条功能标注以及174万个有效的物体-部件-功能链实例。我们进一步引入Level-1到Level-3条件度量指标和ParseRate，用以评估定位精度、跨层级绑定质量和层级完整性。实验表明，现有MLLM和感知拼接管道在我们的SceneParser-Bench上难以处理层级解析任务，而SceneParser在结构感知性能上显著更强。此外，消融实验、在COCO和AGD20K上的评估以及下游规划探测实验证明，SceneParser与常规任务兼容，并为视觉理解提供可操作的层级表征。

</details>


### [83] [Multi-scale Coarse-to-fine Modeling for Test-time Human Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14935)
*Nhat Le,Daochang Liu,Anh Nguyen,Ajmal Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MSCoT提出了一种多尺度粗到细的人体运动合成与控制模型，通过离散化运动为多尺度层级表示、多尺度token引导策略和轻量级token精炼器，实现快速、灵活且高质量的可控文本到运动生成，在HumanML3D上取得SOTA性能（FID提升48%，平均误差降低61%，推理速度提升10倍）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法依赖多轮迭代去噪/Token预测步骤或针对特定控制信号定制的模块，推理速度慢且灵活性不足。MSCoT旨在实现快速、灵活且高质量的运动合成与控制。

Method: 1) 将运动离散化为多尺度层级表示，以粗到细的方式预测每个时间尺度上的完整token序列；2) 提出多尺度token引导策略，克服离散采样挑战，引导token分布朝向控制目标；3) 引入轻量级token精炼器，为离散token嵌入添加连续残差，实现可微分的测试时精炼优化，确保与控制目标精确对齐。

Result: 在HumanML3D上，FID提升48%，平均控制误差降低61%，推理速度比扩散方法快10倍，在可控文本到运动生成任务上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: MSCoT通过多尺度粗到细范式及配套策略，在保证运动质量的同时实现了更快的采样速度和更高的控制精度，为测试时运动合成与控制提供了高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: We present MSCoT, a multi-scale, coarse-to-fine model for test-time human motion synthesis and control. Unlike recent approaches that rely on multiple iterative denoising/token-prediction steps, or modules tailored for specific control signals, MSCoT discretizes motion into a multi-scale hierarchical representation and predicts the entire token sequence at each temporal scale in a coarse-to-fine fashion. Building on this coarse-to-fine paradigm, we propose an efficient multi-scale token guidance strategy that overcomes the challenge of discrete sampling and steers the token distribution towards the control goals, allowing for fast and flexible control. To address the limitations of a discrete codebook, a lightweight token refiner further adds continuous residuals to the discrete token embeddings and allows differentiable test-time refinement optimization to ensure precise alignment with the control objectives. MSCoT is able to produce quality motions, consistent with the control constraints, while offering substantially faster sampling than diffusion-based approaches. Experiments on popular benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art controllable text-to-motion generation performance of MSCoT over existing baselines, with better motion quality (48% FID improvement), higher control accuracy (-61% avg error), and $10 \times$ faster inference speed on HumanML3D.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了MSCoT，一种用于测试时人体运动合成与控制的多尺度粗到细模型。与依赖多轮迭代去噪/Token预测步骤或针对特定控制信号定制模块的最新方法不同，MSCoT将运动离散化为多尺度层级表示，并以粗到细的方式预测每个时间尺度上的完整Token序列。基于这一粗到细范式，我们提出了一种高效的多尺度Token引导策略，克服了离散采样的挑战，将Token分布导向控制目标，从而实现快速灵活的控制。为解决离散码本的局限性，一个轻量级Token精炼器进一步为离散Token嵌入添加连续残差，并允许可微分的测试时精炼优化，以确保与控制目标的精确对齐。MSCoT能够生成与约束条件一致的高质量运动，同时提供比基于扩散的方法快得多的采样速度。在流行基准上的实验表明，MSCoT在可控文本到运动生成任务上优于现有基线，在HumanML3D上实现了更好的运动质量（FID提升48%）、更高的控制精度（平均误差降低61%）以及10倍的推理速度提升。

</details>


### [84] [ACE-LoRA: Adaptive Orthogonal Decoupling for Continual Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14948)
*Yuehao Liu,Weijia Zhang,Xuanming Shang,Zhizhou Chen,Yanhao Ge,Shanyan Guan,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ACE-LoRA动态正则化框架和CIE-Bench基准，解决扩散模型在连续图像编辑中的灾难性遗忘问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际应用要求模型持续适应新任务同时保留旧知识，但连续学习在图像编辑领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: ACE-LoRA框架包含两个核心策略：自适应正交解耦（识别并对齐任务间的参数干扰）和秩不变历史信息压缩（解决持续更新中的可扩展性问题）。同时构建了首个连续图像编辑综合基准CIE-Bench。

Result: 在指令保真度、视觉真实感和抗遗忘鲁棒性方面，ACE-LoRA持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ACE-LoRA建立了连续图像编辑的坚实基础，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题。

Original Abstract: State-of-the-art diffusion models often rely on parameter-efficient fine-tuning to perform specialized image editing tasks. However, real-world applications require continual adaptation to new tasks while preserving previously learned knowledge. Despite the practical necessity, continual learning for image editing remains largely underexplored. We propose ACE-LoRA, a dynamic regularization framework for continual image editing that effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting. ACE-LoRA leverages Adaptive Orthogonal Decoupling to identify and orthogonalize task interference, and introduces a Rank-Invariant Historical Information Compression strategy to address scalability issues in continual updates. To facilitate continual learning in image editing and provide a standardized evaluation protocol, we introduce CIE-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark in this domain. CIE-Bench encompasses diverse and practically relevant image editing scenarios with a balanced level of difficulty to effectively expose limitations of existing models while remaining compatible with parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing baselines in terms of instruction fidelity, visual realism, and robustness to forgetting, establishing a strong foundation for continual learning in image editing.

中文摘要翻译: 最先进的扩散模型通常依赖参数高效微调来执行专门的图像编辑任务。然而，实际应用需要模型持续适应新任务，同时保留先前学到的知识。尽管有实际需求，但图像编辑中的连续学习仍 largely 未被充分探索。我们提出ACE-LoRA，一种面向连续图像编辑的动态正则化框架，能够有效缓解灾难性遗忘。ACE-LoRA利用自适应正交解耦来识别并正交化任务间干扰，并引入秩不变历史信息压缩策略来解决持续更新中的可扩展性问题。为促进图像编辑中的连续学习并提供标准化的评估协议，我们提出了CIE-Bench，该领域首个综合性基准。CIE-Bench涵盖多样化且实际相关的图像编辑场景，具有均衡的难度水平，能有效暴露现有模型的局限性，同时保持与参数高效微调的兼容性。大量实验表明，我们的方法在指令保真度、视觉真实感和抗遗忘鲁棒性方面持续优于现有基线，为图像编辑中的连续学习奠定了坚实基础。

</details>


### [85] [Evo-Depth: A Lightweight Depth-Enhanced Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14950)
*Tao Lin,Yuxin Du,Jiting Liu,Nuobei Zhu,Yunhe Li,Yuqian Fu,Yinxinyu Chen,Hongyi Cai,Zewei Ye,Bing Cheng,Kai Ye,Yiran Mao,Yilei Zhong,MingKang Dong,Junchi Yan,Gen Li,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evo-Depth 提出一种轻量级隐式深度编码框架，仅用多视图 RGB 图像即可增强 VLA 模型的空间感知能力，无需额外传感器，在 0.9B 参数下实现仿真与真实场景的领先性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型依赖 2D 视觉表征，缺乏深度信息与精细空间关系理解；引入显式 3D 输入（深度图/点云）会增加系统复杂度、依赖额外传感器且易受噪声影响；隐式 3D 方法则依赖大型几何基础模型，训练和部署成本高。因此需要一种轻量级、不依赖额外硬件且高效的深度增强方案。

Method: (1) 轻量级隐式深度编码模块 (IDEM)，从多视图 RGB 图像中提取紧凑深度特征；(2) 空间增强模块 (SEM)，通过深度感知调制将深度特征融入视觉-语言表示，实现空间-语义联合增强；(3) 渐进对齐训练策略 (PAS)，将深度增强后的表示与下游动作学习逐步对齐。

Result: 仅 0.9B 参数，在四个仿真基准上取得最优性能；真实实验中取得最高平均成功率，同时模型尺寸最小、GPU 内存占用最低、推理频率最高。

Conclusion: Evo-Depth 通过轻量级隐式深度编码和渐进对齐训练，在不增加额外传感器和部署开销的前提下，有效提升了 VLA 模型的空间操作能力，验证了隐式深度特征在机器人操作中的高效性与实用性。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by unifying perception, language grounding, and action generation. However, they often struggle in scenarios requiring precise spatial understanding, as current VLA models primarily rely on 2D visual representations that lack depth information and detailed spatial relationships. While recent approaches incorporate explicit 3D inputs such as depth maps or point clouds to address this issue, they often increase system complexity, require additional sensors, and remain vulnerable to sensing noise and reconstruction errors. Another line of work explores implicit 3D-aware spatial modeling directly from RGB observations without extra sensors, but it often relies on large geometry foundation models, resulting in higher training and deployment costs. To address these challenges, we propose Evo-Depth, a lightweight depth-enhanced VLA framework that enhances spatially grounded manipulation without relying on additional sensing hardware or compromising deployment efficiency. Evo-Depth employs a lightweight Implicit Depth Encoding Module to extract compact depth features from multi-view RGB images. These features are incorporated into vision-language representations through a Spatial Enhancement Module via depth-aware modulation, enabling efficient spatial-semantic enhancement. A Progressive Alignment Training strategy is further introduced to align the resulting depth-enhanced representations with downstream action learning. With only 0.9B parameters, Evo-Depth achieves superior performance across four simulation benchmarks. In real-world experiments, Evo-Depth attains the highest average success rate while also exhibiting the smallest model size, lowest GPU memory usage, and highest inference frequency among compared methods.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作模型通过统一感知、语言理解和动作生成，已成为机器人操作领域一种有前景的范式。然而，当前 VLA 模型主要依赖缺乏深度信息和细致空间关系的 2D 视觉表征，因此在需要精确空间理解的场景中表现不佳。近期方法通过引入显式 3D 输入（如深度图或点云）来解决该问题，但这通常增加了系统复杂度、需要额外传感器，且易受感知噪声和重建误差的影响。另一条研究路线探索从 RGB 观测中直接进行隐式 3D 感知空间建模，无需额外传感器，但往往依赖大型几何基础模型，导致训练和部署成本较高。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 Evo-Depth——一种轻量级深度增强的 VLA 框架，在无需额外传感硬件或牺牲部署效率的前提下，提升了空间接地操作能力。Evo-Depth 采用轻量级隐式深度编码模块，从多视图 RGB 图像中提取紧凑的深度特征；通过空间增强模块中的深度感知调制将这些特征融入视觉-语言表示，实现高效的空间-语义增强；进一步引入渐进对齐训练策略，将深度增强后的表示与下游动作学习进行对齐。仅凭 0.9B 参数，Evo-Depth 在四个仿真基准上均取得了卓越性能。在真实世界实验中，Evo-Depth 在取得最高平均成功率的同时，在所有对比方法中模型尺寸最小、GPU 内存占用最低、推理频率最高。

</details>


### [86] [H-OmniStereo: Zero-Shot Omnidirectional Stereo Matching with Heading-Aligned Normal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14963)
*Chenxing Jiang,Zhe Tong,Pusen Gao,Peize Liu,Yang Xu,Chuan Fang,Ping Tan,Shaojie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: H-OmniStereo 是一个零样本全向立体匹配框架，通过构建280万+合成数据集和引入航向对齐坐标系下的单目法线估计器，解决了全向立体匹配中数据稀缺和球面畸变导致的单目先验退化问题，在域外数据集和真实消费级相机上实现了更高精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有上下格式等距柱状图的全向立体匹配受限于全向数据集的稀缺性以及球面畸变导致透视单目先验退化，性能严重受限。

Method: (1) 构建包含280万+对上下格式等距柱状图的高质量合成全向立体数据集；(2) 提出在航向对齐坐标系下工作的等距柱状图单目法线估计器，提供抗畸变且跨视图一致的几何先验，提升训练效率并适应训练-测试视场角不匹配问题。

Result: 在域外数据集上达到比现有方法更高的精度，并成功泛化到使用单一模型的真实消费级相机设置。

Conclusion: H-OmniStereo通过大规模合成数据和畸变鲁棒的单目先验有效解决了全向立体匹配中的数据稀缺和畸变问题，实现了零样本泛化。模型和数据集将开源。

Original Abstract: Stereo matching on top-bottom equirectangular images provides an effective framework for full-surround perception, as vertically aligned epipolar lines enable the use of advanced perspective stereo architectures that are largely driven by large-scale datasets and monocular priors. However, the performance of such adaptations is severely limited by the scarcity of omnidirectional stereo datasets and the degradation of perspective monocular priors under spherical distortions.To address these challenges, we propose H-OmniStereo, a zero-shot omnidirectional stereo matching framework. First, we construct high-quality synthetic dataset comprising over 2.8 million top-bottom equirectangular stereo pairs to scale up training. Second, we introduce an equirectangular monocular normal estimator, specifically operating in a heading-aligned coordinate system. Beyond providing distortion-robust and cross-view-consistent geometric priors for establishing reliable correspondences in stereo matching, this design boosts training efficiency and accommodates train-test FoV mismatches.Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves higher accuracy than existing methods on out-of-domain datasets and successfully generalizes to real-world consumer camera setups using a single model. Both the model and the dataset will be open-sourced.

中文摘要翻译: 上下格式等距柱状图上的立体匹配为全环绕感知提供了一种有效框架，因为垂直对齐的极线使得可以利用先进的透视立体架构，这些架构很大程度上由大规模数据集和单目先验驱动。然而，这种适配的性能受到全向立体数据集的稀缺性以及球面畸变下透视单目先验退化的严重限制。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了H-OmniStereo，一种零样本全向立体匹配框架。首先，我们构建了包含超过280万对上下格式等距柱状立体对的高质量合成数据集以扩大训练规模。其次，我们引入了一个等距柱状图单目法线估计器，该估计器在航向对齐坐标系下运行。除了为建立立体匹配中的可靠对应关系提供抗畸变且跨视图一致的几何先验外，该设计还提升了训练效率并适应了训练-测试视场角不匹配问题。大量实验表明，我们的方法在域外数据集上达到了比现有方法更高的精度，并成功使用单一模型泛化到真实消费级相机设置。模型和数据集将进行开源。

</details>


### [87] [MHSA: A Lightweight Framework for Mitigating Hallucinations via Steered Attention in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14966)
*Wei Ding,Yilin Li,Yudong Zhang,Ruobing Xie,Xingwu Sun,Jiansheng Chen,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MHSA框架，通过轻量级MLP生成器校正跨模态注意力模式，在不修改LVLM参数的情况下缓解幻觉，将跨模态注意力机制从幻觉检测扩展到幻觉缓解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LVLM存在幻觉问题，而先前工作DHCP仅从跨模态注意力角度检测幻觉，未解决幻觉缓解问题。

Method: 训练一个三层MLP生成器，在DHCP判别器和LVLM本身的监督信号指导下产生校正后的注意力；推理时直接用校正注意力替换原始跨模态注意力，不修改任何LVLM参数。

Result: 在多种数据集和LVLM上，MHSA能有效缓解判别式和生成式幻觉。

Conclusion: MHSA将跨模态注意力机制从幻觉检测扩展到幻觉缓解，为LVLM幻觉研究提供了新视角，有助于提升模型可靠性。

Original Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across diverse multimodal tasks, yet they continue to suffer from hallucinations, generating content that is inconsistent with the visual input. Prior work DHCP (Detecting Hallucinations by Cross-modal Attention Pattern) has explored hallucination detection from the perspective of cross-modal attention, but does not address hallucination mitigation. In this paper, we propose MHSA (Mitigating Hallucinations via Steered Attention), a lightweight framework that mitigates hallucinations by learning to correct cross-modal attention patterns in LVLMs. MHSA trains a simple three-layer MLP generator to produce corrected attention, guided by supervisory signals from the DHCP discriminator and the LVLM itself. During inference, MHSA mitigates both discriminative and generative hallucinations across various datasets and LVLMs by simply replacing the original cross-modal attention with the corrected one, without modifying any LVLM parameters. By extending cross-modal attention mechanisms from hallucination detection to hallucination mitigation, MHSA offers a novel perspective on hallucination research in LVLMs and helps enhance their reliability.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）在各种多模态任务中取得了显著性能，但仍然存在幻觉问题，即生成与视觉输入不一致的内容。先前的工作DHCP（通过跨模态注意力模式检测幻觉）从跨模态注意力的角度探索了幻觉检测，但未解决幻觉缓解问题。本文提出MHSA（通过引导注意力缓解幻觉），一个轻量级框架，通过学习校正LVLM中的跨模态注意力模式来缓解幻觉。MHSA训练一个简单的三层MLP生成器来产生校正后的注意力，该生成器受DHCP判别器和LVLM本身的监督信号指引。在推理过程中，MHSA通过简单地用校正后的注意力替换原始的跨模态注意力，在不修改任何LVLM参数的情况下，在多种数据集和LVLM上缓解了判别式和生成式幻觉。通过将跨模态注意力机制从幻觉检测扩展到幻觉缓解，MHSA为LVLM幻觉研究提供了新视角，并有助于提升其可靠性。

</details>


### [88] [MicroscopyMatching: Towards a Ready-to-use Framework for Microscopy Image Analysis in Diverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14980)
*Xiaofei Hui,Haoxuan Qu,Hossein Rahmani,Shuohong Wang,Jeff W. Lichtman,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MicroscopyMatching，首个即用型、可跨多种显微镜分析设置可靠执行分割、追踪和计数等关键任务的框架，通过将多样化任务统一重构为匹配问题，并利用预训练潜在扩散模型的鲁棒匹配能力来解决。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 显微镜图像分析在不同设置（生物对象类型、处理协议、成像设备、分析任务等）下存在巨大差异，现有深度学习方法需要大量适配工作，给实验室带来不可持续负担，导致研究者仍依赖人工分析，严重阻碍生物医学研究进展。亟需一个可靠且广泛适用的显微镜图像分析工具。

Method: 将多种显微镜图像分析任务（分割、追踪、计数）统一重构为一个匹配问题，利用预训练潜在扩散模型的鲁棒匹配能力来高效处理该问题。

Result: MicroscopyMatching 是首个即用型显微镜图像分析框架，能够在多样化显微镜分析设置下可靠地执行分割、追踪和计数等关键分析任务。

Conclusion: 通过将任务统一为匹配问题并借助预训练扩散模型的强大泛化能力，该框架有效弥合了现有方法在实际应用中的局限性，为生物医学研究提供了可广泛适用的自动化分析工具。

Original Abstract: Analyzing microscopy images to extract biological object properties (e.g., their morphological organization, temporal dynamics, and population density) is fundamental to various biomedical research. Yet conducting this manually is costly and time-consuming. Though deep learning-based approaches have been explored to automate this process, the substantial diversity of microscopy analysis settings in practice (including variations of biological object types, sample processing protocols, imaging equipment, and analysis tasks, etc.) often renders them ineffective. As a result, these approaches typically require extensive adaptation for different settings, which, however, can impose burdens that are often practically unsustainable for laboratories, forcing biomedical researchers to still commonly rely on manual analysis, thereby severely bottlenecking the pace of biomedical research progress. This situation has created a pressing and long-standing need for a reliable and broadly applicable microscopy image analysis tool, yet such a tool is still missing. To address this gap, we present the first ready-to-use microscopy image analysis framework, MicroscopyMatching, that can reliably perform key analysis tasks (including segmentation, tracking, and counting) across diverse microscopy analysis settings. From a fundamentally different perspective, MicroscopyMatching reformulates diverse microscopy image analysis tasks as a unified matching problem, effectively handling this problem by exploiting the robust matching capability from pre-trained latent diffusion models.

中文摘要翻译: 分析显微镜图像以提取生物对象属性（如其形态组织、时间动态和种群密度）是各类生物医学研究的基础。然而，手动进行这项工作既昂贵又耗时。尽管已有基于深度学习的方法被探索用于自动化这一过程，但实践中显微镜分析设置的高度多样性（包括生物对象类型、样本处理流程、成像设备以及分析任务等的变化）往往使得这些方法效果不佳。因此，这些方法通常需要针对不同设置进行大量适配，而这会给实验室带来实际上难以持续的负担，迫使生物医学研究者仍普遍依赖人工分析，从而严重阻碍了生物医学研究的进展速度。这种情况催生了一个迫切且长期存在的需求——一种可靠且广泛适用的显微镜图像分析工具，但这样的工具迄今仍付之阙如。为填补这一空白，我们提出了首个即用型显微镜图像分析框架 MicroscopyMatching，它能够在多样的显微镜分析设置下可靠地执行关键分析任务（包括分割、追踪和计数）。从一个根本不同的视角出发，MicroscopyMatching 将多样的显微镜图像分析任务重新表述为一个统一的匹配问题，并通过利用预训练潜在扩散模型的鲁棒匹配能力来有效地处理该问题。

</details>


### [89] [Characterizing the visual representation of objects from the child's view](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14990)
*Jane Yang,Tarun Sepuri,Alvin Wei Ming Tan,Khai Loong Aw,Michael C. Frank,Bria Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这项研究分析了婴幼儿第一人称视角的家庭视频数据（BabyView数据集），发现儿童日常视觉经验中物体类别分布极度偏斜（少数类别主导），类别实例高度多变（非典型角度、遮挡等），且动物等多以图像形式出现。令人惊讶的是，尽管存在这些变异性，检测到的类别在上级类别（如动物、食物）内的分组比标准照片数据更强，这一模式在自监督视觉和多模态模型嵌入中均得到复现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究婴幼儿在日常家居环境中视觉经验的具体特征——即类别学习的输入数据性质，包括类别分布、实例变异性以及上级类别结构，从而理解人类视觉类别学习的高效性和鲁棒性基础。

Method: 使用BabyView数据集中31名参与者（5-36个月大）的868小时第一人称视频，对超过300万帧应用监督式目标检测模型提取常见物体类别；并通过自监督视觉模型和多模态模型的高维嵌入分析类别结构。

Result: 1) 儿童视觉经验中物体类别分布高度偏斜（杯子、椅子等少数类别主导）；2) 类别实例高度多变（非典型角度、杂乱场景、部分遮挡）；3) 动物等多以图像/图片形式出现；4) 尽管存在变异性，检测到的类别在上级类别内分组强于标准照片数据；5) 此模式在自监督视觉和多模态模型嵌入以及单个儿童的密集采样数据中均成立。

Conclusion: 理解视觉类别学习的鲁棒性和高效性需要开发能够利用强上级类别结构、并从非典型、稀疏和可变的示例中学习的模型。

Original Abstract: Children acquire object category representations from their everyday experiences in the first few years of life. What do the inputs to this learning process look like? We analyzed first-person videos of young children's visual experience at home from the BabyView dataset ($N$ = 31 participants, 868 hours, ages 5--36 months), using a supervised object detection model to extract common object categories from more than 3 million frames. We found that children's object category exposure was highly skewed: a few categories (e.g., cups, chairs) dominated children's visual experiences while most categories appeared rarely, replicating previous findings from a more restricted set of contexts. Category exemplars were highly variable: children encountered objects from unusual angles, in highly cluttered scenes, and partially occluded views; many categories (especially animals) were most frequently viewed as depictions. Surprisingly, despite this variability, detected categories (e.g., giraffes, apples) showed stronger groupings within superordinate categories (e.g., animals, food) relative to groupings derived from canonical photographs of these categories. We found this same pattern when using high-dimensional embeddings from both self-supervised visual and multimodal models; this effect was also recapitulated in densely sampled data from individual children. Understanding the robustness and efficiency of visual category learning will require the development of models that can exploit strong superordinate structure and learn from non-canonical, sparse, and variable exemplars.
