中文摘要翻译: 儿童在生命最初几年中通过日常经验习得物体类别表征。这一学习过程的输入数据具有怎样的特征？我们利用BabyView数据集中31名参与者（5–36个月大）共计868小时的第一人称家庭视频数据，采用监督式目标检测模型从超过300万帧画面中提取常见物体类别。研究发现，儿童视觉经验中物体类别暴露高度偏斜：少数类别（如杯子、椅子）主导了儿童的视觉体验，而大多数类别出现频率极低，这一结果复现了先前在更有限场景中的发现。类别实例高度多变：儿童常在非典型角度、高度杂乱的场景以及部分遮挡的视角中遇到物体；许多类别（尤其是动物）最常见的形式是图像/图片。令人惊讶的是，尽管存在这种变异性，检测到的类别（如长颈鹿、苹果）在上级类别（如动物、食物）内部的分组能力强于从这些类别的标准照片数据中得出的分组。我们在自监督视觉模型和多模态模型的高维嵌入中发现了相同的模式；这一效应也在单个儿童密集采样的数据中得以复现。理解视觉类别学习的鲁棒性和高效性需要开发能够利用强上级类别结构、并从非典型、稀疏和可变的示例中学习的模型。

</details>


### [90] [Predicting Response to Neoadjuvant Chemotherapy in Ovarian Cancer from CT Baseline Using Multi-Loss Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14991)
*Francesco Pastori,Francesca Fati,Marina Rosanu,Luigi De Vitis,Lucia Ribero,Gabriella Schivardi,Giovanni Damiano Aletti,Nicoletta Colombo,Jvan Casarin,Francesco Multinu,Elena De Momi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的非侵入性框架，利用治疗前对比增强CT图像预测卵巢癌患者对新辅助化疗的反应，在280例患者队列上取得ROC-AUC 0.73和F1-score 0.70的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卵巢癌是最致命的妇科恶性肿瘤，约60%患者确诊时已处于晚期，5年生存率仅约30%。早期识别新辅助化疗无反应者是一个关键的未满足临床需求，这可以避免无效治疗并防止延误最佳手术时机。

Method: 提出非侵入性深度学习框架：从治疗前对比增强CT中自动提取3D病灶掩膜，使用部分微调的预训练图像编码器编码轴向切片，通过基于注意力的模块将切片级表示聚合为体素嵌入。训练结合分类损失、监督对比正则化和难负样本挖掘，以提高反应者与无反应者之间的区分度。

Result: 在米兰欧洲肿瘤研究所280例患者（147例反应者，133例无反应者）的单中心回顾性队列上，测试集ROC-AUC为0.73（95% CI: 0.58-0.86），F1-score为0.70（95% CI: 0.56-0.82）。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构能够学习临床相关的预测模式，为基于影像学的卵巢癌新辅助化疗反应分层工具提供了稳健基础。

Original Abstract: Ovarian cancer is the most lethal gynecologic malignancy: around 60% of patients are diagnosed at an advanced stage, with an associated 5-year survival rate of about 30%. Early identification of non-responders to neoadjuvant chemotherapy remains a key unmet need, as it could prevent ineffective therapy and avoid delays in optimal surgical management. This work proposes a non-invasive deep learning framework to predict neoadjuvant chemotherapy response from pre-treatment contrast-enhanced CT by leveraging automatically derived 3D lesion masks. The approach encodes axial slices with a partially fine-tuned pretrained image encoder and aggregates slice-level representations into a volumetric embedding through an attention-based module. Training combines classification loss with supervised contrastive regularization and hard-negative mining to improve separation between ambiguous responders and non-responders. The method was developed on a retrospective single-center cohort from the European Institute of Oncology (Milan, IT), including 280 eligible patients (147 responder, 133 non-responder). On the test cohort, the model achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.73 (95% CI: 0.58-0.86) and an F1-score of 0.70 (95% CI: 0.56-0.82). Overall, these results suggest that the proposed architecture learns clinically relevant predictive patterns and provides a robust foundation for an imaging-based stratification tool.

中文摘要翻译: 卵巢癌是最致命的妇科恶性肿瘤：约60%的患者在确诊时已处于晚期，其五年生存率约为30%。早期识别新辅助化疗的无反应者仍是一个关键的未满足需求，因为这可以避免无效治疗并防止延误最佳手术时机。本文提出了一种非侵入性深度学习框架，利用治疗前对比增强CT，通过自动推导的3D病灶掩膜来预测新辅助化疗反应。该方法使用部分微调的预训练图像编码器对轴向切片进行编码，并通过基于注意力的模块将切片级表示聚合为体素嵌入。训练过程结合了分类损失与监督对比正则化及难负样本挖掘，以改善对模棱两可的反应者与无反应者的区分。该方法基于米兰欧洲肿瘤研究所的单中心回顾性队列进行开发，共纳入280例合格患者（147例反应者，133例无反应者）。在测试队列上，模型达到了0.73的ROC-AUC（95%置信区间：0.58-0.86）和0.70的F1分数（95%置信区间：0.56-0.82）。总体而言，这些结果表明所提出的架构学习了临床相关的预测模式，并为基于影像学的分层工具提供了稳健基础。

</details>


### [91] [3D Skew-Normal Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15010)
*Xiangru Wu,Ke Fan,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Skew-Normal Splatting (SNS)，用Azzalini偏态正态分布替代高斯分布作为3D高斯溅射的基本图元，通过可学习的偏度参数实现从对称高斯到半高斯形状的连续插值，在不增加额外计算负担的情况下提升重建质量，尤其擅长处理尖锐边界和薄/单侧结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统3D高斯溅射使用对称高斯原语，在有限图元预算下对非对称结构（如物体边界和单侧表面）的表达不够紧凑。现有改进方法要么仍局限于椭圆族分布，要么依赖硬截断导致形状控制不连续，缺乏灵活的形状调控能力。

Method: 提出Skew-Normal Splatting方法，采用Azzalini偏态正态分布作为基本图元，引入可学习且有界偏度参数实现对称高斯到半高斯形状的连续插值；该方法在仿射变换和边缘化下保持解析可解性，可无缝集成到现有高斯溅射光栅化管线；同时提出解耦参数化和块状优化策略，解决尺度、旋转和偏度参数间的强耦合问题，提升训练稳定性与精度。

Result: 在新视角合成标准基准上的大量实验表明，SNS在重建质量上持续优于高斯核和近期非高斯核方法，在尖锐边界、薄结构和单侧结构上改进尤为明显。

Conclusion: SNS通过引入偏态正态分布作为基本原语，实现了更灵活的形状建模能力，在保持计算效率和管线兼容性的同时显著提升了3D场景重建质量，尤其适合处理具有非对称几何结构的场景。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a leading representation for real-time novel view synthesis and been widely adopted in various downstream applications. The core strength of 3DGS lies in its efficient kernel-based scene representation, where Gaussian primitives provide favorable mathematical and computational properties. However, under a finite primitive budget, the symmetric shape of each primitive directly affects representation compactness, especially near asymmetric structures such as object boundaries and one-sided surfaces. Recent works have explored more complex kernel distributions, yet they either remain within the elliptical family or rely on hard truncation, which limits continuous shape control and introduces distributional discontinuities. In this paper, we propose Skew-Normal Splatting (SNS), which adopts the Azzalini Skew-Normal distribution as the fundamental primitive. By introducing a learnable and bounded skewness parameter, SNS can continuously interpolate between symmetric Gaussians and Half-Gaussian-like shapes, enabling flexible modeling of both sharp boundaries and interior regions. Moremover, SNS preserves analytical tractability under affine transformations and marginalization. This property allows seamless integration into existing Gaussian Splatting rasterization pipelines.Furthermore, to address the strong coupling between scale, rotation, and skewness parameters, we introduce a decoupled parameterization and a block-wise optimization strategy to enhance training stability and accuracy. Extensive experiments on standard novel-view synthesis benchmarks show that SNS consistently improves reconstruction quality over Gaussian and recent non-Gaussian kernels, with clearer benefits on sharp boundaries and thin or one-sided structures.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯溅射（3DGS）已成为实时新视角合成的领先表示方法，并被广泛用于各种下游应用。3DGS的核心优势在于其高效的基于核的场景表示，其中高斯原语提供了有利的数学和计算特性。然而，在有限的原语预算下，每个原语的对称形状直接影响表示紧凑性，尤其是在物体边界和单侧表面等非对称结构附近。近期研究探索了更复杂的核分布，但它们要么仍局限于椭圆族，要么依赖硬截断，这限制了连续的形状控制并引入了分布不连续性。本文提出偏态正态溅射（Skew-Normal Splatting, SNS），采用Azzalini偏态正态分布作为基本原语。通过引入可学习且有界的偏度参数，SNS可以在对称高斯与半高斯形状之间连续插值，从而灵活地建模尖锐边界和内部区域。此外，SNS在仿射变换和边缘化下保持解析可解性，这一特性使其能够无缝集成到现有的高斯溅射光栅化管线中。进一步地，针对尺度、旋转和偏度参数之间的强耦合问题，我们引入了解耦参数化和块状优化策略，以增强训练的稳定性和精度。在新视角合成标准基准上的大量实验表明，SNS在重建质量上持续优于高斯核和近期非高斯核方法，在尖锐边界、薄结构或单侧结构上的改进尤为明显。

</details>


### [92] [EverAnimate: Minute-Scale Human Animation via Latent Flow Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15042)
*Wuyang Li,Yang Gao,Mariam Hassan,Lan Feng,Wentao Pan,Po-Chien Luan,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EverAnimate提出了一种高效的后期训练方法，通过持久性潜在上下文记忆（包括持久性潜在传播和修复性流匹配）解决长时程动画视频生成中的累积漂移问题，在10秒和90秒设置下均显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长时程动画中需要合成高度动态的人体运动与相对静态的环境，导致基于分块的生成存在累积漂移，包括低层质量漂移（如静态背景逐渐退化）和高层语义漂移（如角色身份和视角相关属性不一致）。现有方法难以同时解决这两类漂移问题。

Method: EverAnimate包含两个互补机制：(1) 持久性潜在传播——在分块间维护上下文记忆，在潜在空间中传播身份和运动信息，缓解时序遗忘；(2) 修复性流匹配——在采样过程中通过速度调整引入隐式修复目标，提升分块内部的生成保真度。仅需轻量级LoRA微调即可实现。

Result: 在10秒设置下，PSNR/SSIM提升8%/7%，LPIPS/FID降低22%/11%；在90秒设置下，PSNR/SSIM提升15%/15%，LPIPS/FID降低32%/27%，均优于最先进的长视频动画方法。

Conclusion: EverAnimate通过持久性潜在上下文记忆机制有效解决了长时程动画中的累积漂移问题，仅需轻量级LoRA微调即可在短时程和长时程场景下均取得显著性能提升，验证了所提方法的有效性和高效性。

Original Abstract: We propose EverAnimate, an efficient post-training method for long-horizon animated video generation that preserves visual quality and character identity. Long-form animation remains challenging because highly dynamic human motion must be synthesized against relatively static environments, making chunk-based generation prone to accumulated drift: (i) low-level quality drift, such as progressive degradation of static backgrounds, and (ii) high-level semantic drift, such as inconsistent character identity and view-dependent attributes. To address this issue, EverAnimate restores drifted flow trajectories by anchoring generation to a persistent latent context memory, consisting of two complementary mechanisms. (i) Persistent Latent Propagation maintains a context memory across chunks to propagate identity and motion in latent space while mitigating temporal forgetting. (ii) Restorative Flow Matching introduces an implicit restoration objective during sampling through velocity adjustment, improving within-chunk fidelity. With only lightweight LoRA tuning, EverAnimate outperforms state-of-the-art long-animation methods in both short- and long-horizon settings: at 10 seconds, it improves PSNR/SSIM by 8%/7% and reduces LPIPS/FID by 22%/11%; at 90 seconds, the gains increase to 15%/15% and 32%/27%, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了EverAnimate，一种高效的后训练方法，用于长时程动画视频生成，能够保持视觉质量和角色身份。长格式动画仍然具有挑战性，因为高度动态的人体运动必须在相对静态的环境中合成，这使得基于分块的生成容易产生累积漂移：（i）低层质量漂移，例如静态背景的逐渐退化，以及（ii）高层语义漂移，例如角色身份和视角相关属性的不一致。为了解决这一问题，EverAnimate通过将生成锚定到持久性潜在上下文记忆来修复漂移的流轨迹，该记忆由两种互补机制组成。（i）持久性潜在传播在分块之间维护上下文记忆，以在潜在空间中传播身份和运动，同时缓解时序遗忘。（ii）修复性流匹配通过速度调整在采样过程中引入隐式修复目标，提升分块内部的保真度。仅通过轻量级LoRA微调，EverAnimate在短时程和长时程设置下均优于最先进的长动画方法：在10秒时，PSNR/SSIM提升8%/7%，LPIPS/FID降低22%/11%；在90秒时，增益分别提升至15%/15%和32%/27%。

</details>


### [93] [LATERN: Test-Time Context-Aware Explainable Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15054)
*Mitchell Piehl,Muchao Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LATERN框架，通过上下文感知异常评分（CEA）和递归证据聚合（REA）两个模块，将视频异常检测（VAD）重构为时序证据聚合过程，使冻结的视觉-语言模型（VLM）能够生成时序连贯且语义可解释的事件级异常检测结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于VLM的视频异常检测方法受限于token约束，只能进行独立的片段级推理，缺乏结构化时序上下文，导致预测和解释碎片化。本文旨在让VLM能够将异常解释为偏离动态演变视频模式的偏差，从而生成连贯的检测结果。

Method: 提出LATERN框架，包含两个互补模块：（1）上下文感知异常评分（CEA），通过基于图像的记忆机制，利用帧多样性和视觉-文本对齐选择性选取历史内容作为扩展上下文，生成可靠异常分数；（2）递归证据聚合（REA），基于异常分数进行递归时序聚合，识别连续的异常区间，并生成基于视觉-文本证据的事件级决策和解释。

Result: 在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence等挑战性基准数据集上的大量实验表明，LATERN在测试时能够提升冻结VLM的检测准确率和解释一致性，同时生成时序连贯且语义可解释的事件级解释。

Conclusion: LATERN通过时序证据聚合机制有效解决了VLM在视频异常检测中缺乏时序上下文的问题，在不微调模型的前提下提升了检测性能和解释质量。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for video anomaly detection (VAD) due to their strong visual reasoning ability and natural language-based explainability. In this paper, we aim to address a key limitation of such pipelines, which perform segment-level inference independently owing to token constraints and reason without structured temporal context, allowing VLMs to interpret anomalies as deviations from evolving video dynamics rather than producing fragmented predictions and explanations. To specify, we propose a context-aware framework named LATERN, which reformulates VAD as a temporal evidence aggregation process. LATERN consists of two complementary modules: Context-Aware Anomaly Scoring (CEA) and Recursive Evidence Aggregation (REA). CEA introduces a novel image-grounded memory mechanism, which selectively chooses historical content via frame diversity and visual-textual alignment as expanded context to help generate reliable anomaly scores. Building upon these scores, REA performs recursive temporal aggregation to identify coherent anomaly intervals and produce event-level decisions and explanations grounded in visual-textual evidence. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks, including UCF-Crime and XD-Violence, show that LATERN enhances detection accuracy and explanation consistency for frozen VLMs during test time, while generating temporally coherent and semantically grounded event-level explanations.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）因其强大的视觉推理能力和基于自然语言的可解释性，近期已成为视频异常检测（VAD）的一种有前景的范式。本文旨在解决此类流水线的一个关键局限性：由于token约束，它们只能独立执行片段级推理，且推理过程缺乏结构化的时序上下文。本文使VLM能够将异常解释为偏离动态演变的视频模式的偏差，从而产生碎片化的预测和解释。具体而言，我们提出了一种名为LATERN的上下文感知框架，该框架将VAD重新构建为时序证据聚合过程。LATERN由两个互补模块组成：上下文感知异常评分（CEA）和递归证据聚合（REA）。CEA引入了一种新颖的基于图像的记忆机制，通过帧多样性和视觉-文本对齐选择性选取历史内容作为扩展上下文，以帮助生成可靠的异常分数。基于这些分数，REA执行递归时序聚合，以识别连贯的异常区间，并生成基于视觉-文本证据的事件级决策和解释。在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence等挑战性基准上的大量实验表明，LATERN在测试时提升了冻结VLM的检测准确率和解释一致性，同时生成了时序连贯且语义可解释的事件级解释。

</details>


### [94] [On the Cultural Anachronism and Temporal Reasoning in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15071)
*Mukul Ranjan,Prince Jha,Khushboo Kumari,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现并提出视觉语言模型在解读历史文物时存在“文化时代错位”现象（即使用时序不当的概念或框架错误解释历史物品），为此构建了TAB-VLM基准数据集（600道题、6个类别、涵盖印度1600件文物），系统评估10个顶尖模型，最佳模型GPT-5.2仅达到58.7%的准确率，表明当前VLM在文化遗产时序推理方面存在显著缺陷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉语言模型在文化遗产材料（数字档案、教育平台等）中的应用日益广泛，但现有模型在解读历史文物时存在一种根本性问题——倾向于使用时序不当的概念或文化框架错误解释历史物品。作者将这一现象定义为“文化时代错位”（cultural anachronism），并旨在量化评估该问题。

Method: 构建了Temporal Anachronism Benchmark for Vision-Language Models (TAB-VLM)，包含600道问题、6个类别，覆盖1,600件印度文化文物（从史前到现代）。在10个SOTA视觉语言模型上进行系统评估。

Result: 最佳模型GPT-5.2仅达到58.7%的整体准确率；性能差距在不同架构和模型规模下持续存在；表明文化时代错位是视觉AI系统的一个显著局限性，且不受模型大小影响。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型的能力与准确解读文化遗产材料所需的能力之间存在显著差距，尤其对于训练数据中代表性不足的非西方视觉文化。TAB-VLM基准为增强多模态AI系统处理历史文物时的时序认知能力提供了基础。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to cultural heritage materials, from digital archives to educational platforms. This work identifies a fundamental issue in how these models interpret historical artifacts. We define this phenomenon as cultural anachronism, the tendency to misinterpret historical objects using temporally inappropriate concepts, materials, or cultural frameworks. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the Temporal Anachronism Benchmark for Vision-Language Models (TAB-VLM), a dataset of 600 questions across six categories, designed to evaluate temporal reasoning on 1,600 Indian cultural artifacts spanning prehistoric to modern periods. Systematic evaluations of ten state-of-the-art models reveal significant deficiencies on our benchmark, and even the best model (GPT-5.2) achieves only 58.7% overall accuracy. The performance gap persists across varying architectures and scales, suggesting that cultural anachronism represents a significant limitation in visual AI systems, regardless of model size. These findings highlight the disparity between current VLM capabilities and the requirements for accurately interpreting cultural heritage materials, particularly for non-Western visual cultures underrepresented in training data. Our benchmark provides a foundation for enhancing temporal cognition in multimodal AI systems that interact with historical artifacts. The dataset and code are available in our project page.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）正越来越多地应用于文化遗产材料，从数字档案到教育平台。本文识别出这些模型在解读历史文物时的一个根本性问题。我们将这一现象定义为文化时代错位（cultural anachronism），即使用时序不当的概念、材料或文化框架错误解释历史物品的倾向。为量化这一现象，我们提出了面向视觉语言模型的时间错位基准（Temporal Anachronism Benchmark for Vision-Language Models, TAB-VLM），这是一个包含600道问题、覆盖6个类别的数据集，旨在评估对跨越史前到现代1,600件印度文化文物的时序推理能力。对10个最先进模型的系统评估揭示了我们的基准上的显著不足，即使是最好的模型（GPT-5.2）也仅达到58.7%的整体准确率。这一性能差距在不同架构和模型规模下持续存在，表明文化时代错位是视觉AI系统的一个显著局限性，且不受模型大小影响。这些发现凸显了当前VLM能力与准确解读文化遗产材料所需能力之间的差距，尤其是对于训练数据中代表性不足的非西方视觉文化。我们的基准为增强与历史文物交互的多模态AI系统的时序认知能力提供了基础。数据集和代码可在我们的项目页面获取。

</details>


### [95] [DriveCtrl: Conditioned Sim-to-Real Driving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15116)
*Haonan Zhao,Yiting Wang,Jingkun Chen,Valentina Donzella,Thomas Bashford-Rogers,Kurt Debattista*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveCtrl是一个基于深度条件控制的仿真到真实视频生成框架，通过结构感知适配器保持场景布局和运动模式，利用可扩展数据生成流水线将仿真视频转换为逼真驾驶视频，并提出了驾驶视频真实感评分（DVRS）评估指标，显著缩小了仿真与真实驾驶视频之间的域差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模标注的驾驶视频数据对训练自动驾驶系统至关重要，但仿真数据与真实世界驾驶视频之间存在显著的域差距，限制了仿真数据的实际应用。现有视频生成方法无法同时保留场景结构、物体动态、时间一致性和视觉真实感，而这些对于保持生成数据中标注的有效性至关重要。

Method: DriveCtrl基于预训练的视频基础模型，引入结构感知适配器实现深度引导生成，同时保留源仿真的场景布局和运动模式。该方法提出了一个可扩展的数据生成流水线，将仿真视频转换为匹配目标真实数据集视觉风格的真实驾驶视频，支持结构深度、参考数据集风格和文本提示三种条件信号，同时保留帧级标注用于下游感知任务。此外，提出了驾驶领域知识驱动的评估指标DVRS来评估生成视频的真实感。

Result: 实验表明DriveCtrl在真实感、时间质量和感知任务性能方面 consistently 优于基础模型和竞争方法，显著缩小了驾驶视频生成的仿真到真实域差距。

Conclusion: DriveCtrl通过深度条件可控的仿真到真实视频生成框架，有效解决了仿真驾驶视频与真实视频之间的域差距问题，为自动驾驶系统训练提供了可扩展的高质量数据生成方案。

Original Abstract: Large-scale labelled driving video data is essential for training autonomous driving systems. Although simulation offers scalable and fully annotated data, the domain gap between synthetic and real-world driving videos significantly limits its utility for downstream deployment. Existing video generation methods are not well-suited for this task, as they fail to simultaneously preserve scene structure, object dynamics, temporal consistency, and visual realism, all of which are critical for maintaining annotation validity in generated data. In this paper, we present DriveCtrl, a depth-conditioned controllable sim-to-real video generation framework for realistic driving video synthesis. Built upon a pretrained video foundation model, DriveCtrl introduces a structure-aware adapter that enables depth-guided generation while preserving the scene layout and motion patterns of the source simulation, producing temporally coherent driving videos that remain aligned with the original simulated sequences. We further introduce a scalable data generation pipeline that transforms simulator videos into realistic driving footage matching the visual style of a target real-world dataset. The pipeline supports three conditioning signals: structural depth, reference-dataset style, and text prompts, while preserving frame-level annotations for downstream perception tasks. To better assess this task, we propose a driving-domain-specific knowledge-informed evaluation metric called Driving Video Realism Score (DVRS) that assesses the realism of generated videos. Experiments demonstrate that DriveCtrl consistently outperforms the base model and competing alternatives in realism, temporal quality, and perception task performance, substantially narrowing the sim-to-real gap for driving video generation.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模标注的驾驶视频数据对于训练自动驾驶系统至关重要。尽管仿真可以提供可扩展且完全标注的数据，但合成驾驶视频与真实世界驾驶视频之间的域差距显著限制了其在下游部署中的实用性。现有的视频生成方法并不适合这一任务，因为它们无法同时保留场景结构、物体动态、时间一致性和视觉真实感——而所有这些对于保持生成数据中标注的有效性都至关重要。在本文中，我们提出了DriveCtrl，一种基于深度条件的可控仿真到真实视频生成框架，用于合成逼真的驾驶视频。DriveCtrl构建于预训练的视频基础模型之上，引入了一个结构感知适配器，能够在保持源仿真的场景布局和运动模式的同时实现深度引导生成，生成与原始仿真序列保持对齐的时间一致驾驶视频。我们进一步提出了一种可扩展的数据生成流水线，将仿真视频转换为与目标真实数据集视觉风格相匹配的真实驾驶视频。该流水线支持三种条件信号：结构深度、参考数据集风格和文本提示，同时保留帧级标注用于下游感知任务。为了更好地评估该任务，我们提出了一种驾驶领域知识驱动的评估指标——驾驶视频真实感评分（DVRS），用于评估生成视频的真实感。实验表明，DriveCtrl在真实感、时间质量和感知任务性能方面 consistently 优于基础模型和竞争方案，显著缩小了驾驶视频生成的仿真到真实域差距。

</details>


### [96] [Causal Forcing++: Scalable Few-Step Autoregressive Diffusion Distillation for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15141)
*Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Kaiwen Zheng,Zihan Zhou,Bokai Yan,Xinyuan Li,Xiao Yang,Chongxuan Li,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Causal Forcing++，一种通过因果一致性蒸馏（Causal CD）实现帧级1-2步自回归视频生成的框架，在降低延迟和训练成本的同时，在VBench、VisionReward等指标上超越SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自回归扩散蒸馏方法在逐块4步生成上表现良好，但存在响应粒度粗、采样延迟高等问题。本文希望在更激进的帧级1-2步自回归设定下突破瓶颈，解决初始化策略与目标不对齐、无法支持少步生成或扩展成本过高的问题。

Method: 提出Causal Forcing++，核心是因果一致性蒸馏（Causal CD）：学习与因果ODE蒸馏相同的AR条件流映射，但仅需相邻时间步之间单个在线教师ODE步的监督，无需预计算和存储完整PF-ODE轨迹，从而高效、可扩展地初始化少步AR学生模型。

Result: 在帧级2步设定下，超越SOTA的4步逐块Causal Forcing：VBench Total提升0.1，VBench Quality提升0.3，VisionReward提升0.335；首帧延迟降低50%，Stage 2训练成本减少约4倍。并扩展到Genie3风格的行动条件世界模型生成。

Conclusion: Causal Forcing++通过因果一致性蒸馏实现了高效的帧级少步自回归视频生成，在性能、延迟和训练效率上均显著优于现有方法，并具备良好的扩展性。

Original Abstract: Real-time interactive video generation requires low-latency, streaming, and controllable rollout. Existing autoregressive (AR) diffusion distillation methods have achieved strong results in the chunk-wise 4-step regime by distilling bidirectional base models into few-step AR students, but they remain limited by coarse response granularity and non-negligible sampling latency. In this paper, we study a more aggressive setting: frame-wise autoregression with only 1--2 sampling steps. In this regime, we identify the initialization of a few-step AR student as the key bottleneck: existing strategies are either target-misaligned, incapable of few-step generation, or too costly to scale. We propose \textbf{Causal Forcing++}, a principled and scalable pipeline that uses \emph{causal consistency distillation} (causal CD) for few-step AR initialization. The core idea is that causal CD learns the same AR-conditional flow map as causal ODE distillation, but obtains supervision from a single online teacher ODE step between adjacent timesteps, avoiding the need to precompute and store full PF-ODE trajectories. This makes the initialization both more efficient and easier to optimize. The resulting pipeline, \ours, surpasses the SOTA 4-step chunk-wise Causal Forcing under the \textit{\textbf{frame-wise 2-step setting}} by 0.1 in VBench Total, 0.3 in VBench Quality, and 0.335 in VisionReward, while reducing first-frame latency by 50\% and Stage 2 training cost by $\sim$$4\times$. We further extend the pipeline to action-conditioned world model generation in the spirit of Genie3. Project Page: https://github.com/thu-ml/Causal-Forcing and https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM .

中文摘要翻译: 实时交互式视频生成需要低延迟、流式且可控的展开。现有的自回归（AR）扩散蒸馏方法通过将双向基模型蒸馏为少步AR学生模型，在逐块4步生成机制中取得了强劲的结果，但仍受限于粗粒度的响应粒度和不可忽视的采样延迟。在本文中，我们研究了一个更具挑战性的设定：帧级自回归，仅需1-2步采样。在此机制下，我们识别出少步AR学生模型的初始化是关键瓶颈：现有策略要么与目标不对齐，要么无法支持少步生成，要么扩展成本过高。我们提出Causal Forcing++，一个原理性且可扩展的流水线，使用因果一致性蒸馏（Causal CD）进行少步AR初始化。其核心思想是：Causal CD学习与因果ODE蒸馏相同的AR条件流映射，但仅需从相邻时间步之间的单个在线教师ODE步获取监督，避免了预计算和存储完整PF-ODE轨迹的需求。这使得初始化既更高效又更易于优化。由此产生的流水线\ours在帧级2步设定下，在VBench Total上超越SOTA的4步逐块Causal Forcing 0.1，在VBench Quality上超越0.3，在VisionReward上超越0.335，同时首帧延迟降低50%，Stage 2训练成本减少约4倍。我们进一步将该流水线扩展到Genie3风格的行动条件世界模型生成。

</details>


### [97] [Does Synthetic Layered Design Data Benefit Layered Design Decomposition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15167)
*Kam Man Wu,Haolin Yang,Qingyu Chen,Yihu Tang,Jingye Chen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究纯合成分层数据是否能提升图形设计分解效果。基于CLD基线构建SynLayers合成数据集，利用视觉语言模型生成文本监督和自动化推理输入。实验表明：纯合成数据训练可超越真实数据集；性能随数据量增益在5万样本左右趋缓；合成数据能避免真实数据中图层数量不平衡问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像生成技术虽能产生高质量图像，但输出是扁平化的，前景、背景和文字纠缠在同一画布上，导致生成后编辑困难。现有方法依赖稀缺的商业分层资产或从有限结构先验构建部分合成数据，均面临可扩展性挑战。本文探索纯合成分层数据是否能作为可扩展且有效的替代方案。

Method: 基于CLD（SOTA图层分解框架）基线，构建纯合成数据集SynLayers；使用视觉语言模型（VLM）生成文本监督信息；利用VLM预测的边界框自动化推理输入。通过数据为中心的实验，系统研究合成数据在图形设计分解中的有效性。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1) 纯合成数据训练可超越非可扩展方案（如PrismLayersPro数据集）；(2) 性能随训练数据规模增加而提升，但在约5万样本时增益开始饱和；(3) 合成数据能实现图层数量分布的平衡控制，避免真实数据中常见的图层数量不平衡问题。

Conclusion: 纯合成分层数据可作为图形设计分解任务的有效且可扩展的基础，有望促进合成数据在分层设计编辑系统中的更广泛采用。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in image generation have made it easy to produce high-quality images. However, these outputs are inherently flattened, entangling foreground elements, background, and text within a fixed canvas. As a result, flexible post-generation editing remains challenging, revealing a clear last-mile gap toward practical usability. Existing approaches either rely on scarce proprietary layered assets or construct partially synthetic data from limited structural priors. However, both strategies face fundamental challenges in scalability. In this work, we investigate whether pure synthetic layered data can improve graphic design decomposition. We make the assumption that, in graphic design, effective decomposition does not require modeling inter-layer dependencies as precisely as in natural-image composition, since design elements are often intentionally arranged as modular and semantically separable components. Concretely, we conduct a data-centric study based on CLD baseline, which is a state-of-the-art layer decomposition framework. Based on the baseline, we construct our own synthetic dataset, SynLayers, generate textual supervision using vision language models, and automate inference inputs with VLM-predicted bounding boxes. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) even training with purely synthetic data can outperform non-scalable alternatives such as the widely used PrismLayersPro dataset, demonstrating its viability as a scalable and effective substitute; (2) performance consistently improves with increased training data scale, while gains begin to saturate at around 50K samples; and (3) synthetic data enables balanced control over layer-count distributions, avoiding the layer-count imbalance commonly observed in real-world datasets. We hope this data-centric study encourages broader adoption of synthetic data as a practical foundation for layered design editing systems.

中文摘要翻译: 图像生成领域的最新进展使得高质量图像的生成变得容易。然而，这些输出本质上是扁平化的，前景元素、背景和文本纠缠在一个固定画布上。因此，灵活的生成后编辑仍然具有挑战性，暴露出通往实际可用性的明显最后一英里差距。现有方法要么依赖稀缺的商业分层资产，要么从有限的结构先验构建部分合成数据。然而，这两种策略都面临着可扩展性方面的根本挑战。在这项工作中，我们研究了纯合成分层数据能否改进图形设计分解。我们假设，在图形设计中，有效的分解不需要像自然图像合成那样精确建模层间依赖关系，因为设计元素通常被有意安排为模块化和语义可分离的组件。具体而言，我们基于CLD基线（一种最先进的图层分解框架）进行了一项以数据为中心的研究。在该基线基础上，我们构建了自己的合成数据集SynLayers，利用视觉语言模型生成文本监督，并使用VLM预测的边界框自动化推理输入。我们的研究揭示了三个关键发现：(1) 即使使用纯合成数据训练，也能超越非可扩展的替代方案（如广泛使用的PrismLayersPro数据集），证明了其作为可扩展且有效的替代方案的可行性；(2) 性能随着训练数据规模的增加而持续提升，但在约5万个样本时增益开始趋于饱和；(3) 合成数据能够实现对图层数量分布的平衡控制，避免了真实数据集中常见的图层数量不平衡问题。我们希望这项以数据为中心的研究能够鼓励更广泛地采用合成数据作为分层设计编辑系统的实用基础。

</details>


### [98] [Evidential Reasoning Advances Interpretable Real-World Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15171)
*Chenyu Lian,Hong-Yu Zhou,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviScreen是一个基于证据推理的疾病筛查框架，通过从双知识库检索历史病例的区域级证据，实现可回溯可定位的透明推理，在临床级召回率下显著提升特异性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有疾病筛查模型存在两个主要问题：一是缺乏可解释性，无法提供透明的推理路径；二是性能欠佳，缺乏有效机制来参考历史病例以辅助判断。

Method: 提出EviScreen框架，包含三个关键模块：(1) 双知识库存储区域级历史证据；(2) 证据感知推理模块，结合当前病例与历史病例证据进行预测；(3) 基于对比检索的异常图（abnormality maps）实现定位可解释性，替代传统事后显著性图。

Result: 在真实疾病筛查基准上取得优越性能，在临床级召回率下实现了显著更高的特异性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: EviScreen通过引入历史病例证据和对比检索机制，同时提升了疾病筛查的性能和可解释性（包括回溯可解释性和定位可解释性），是一种有效的临床筛查框架。

Original Abstract: Disease screening is critical for early detection and timely intervention in clinical practice. However, most current screening models for medical images suffer from limited interpretability and suboptimal performance. They often lack effective mechanisms to reference historical cases or provide transparent reasoning pathways. To address these challenges, we introduce EviScreen, an evidential reasoning framework for disease screening that leverages region-level evidence from historical cases. The proposed EviScreen offers retrospection interpretability through regional evidence retrieved from dual knowledge banks. Using this evidential mechanism, the subsequent evidence-aware reasoning module makes predictions using both the current case and evidence from historical cases, thereby enhancing disease screening performance. Furthermore, rather than relying on post-hoc saliency maps, EviScreen enhances localization interpretability by leveraging abnormality maps derived from contrastive retrieval. Our method achieves superior performance on our carefully established benchmarks for real-world disease screening, yielding notably higher specificity at clinical-level recall. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen.

中文摘要翻译: 疾病筛查对于临床实践中的早期检测和及时干预至关重要。然而，当前大多数医学图像筛查模型存在可解释性有限和性能欠佳的问题。它们往往缺乏有效机制来参考历史病例或提供透明的推理路径。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了EviScreen——一个基于证据推理的疾病筛查框架，该框架利用来自历史病例的区域级证据。所提出的EviScreen通过从双知识库中检索区域证据实现回溯可解释性。利用这一证据机制，后续的证据感知推理模块同时使用当前病例和历史病例中的证据进行预测，从而提升疾病筛查性能。此外，EviScreen不依赖事后显著性图，而是利用对比检索生成的异常图来增强定位可解释性。我们的方法在我们精心构建的真实疾病筛查基准上取得了优越性能，在临床级召回率下实现了显著更高的特异性。代码已开源，地址为https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen。

</details>


### [99] [SANA-WM: Efficient Minute-Scale World Modeling with Hybrid Linear Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15178)
*Haoyi Zhu,Haozhe Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Tian Ye,Junsong Chen,Jincheng Yu,Tong He,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-WM 是一个 2.6B 参数的开源世界模型，能够高效生成 720p、分钟级视频，并实现精确相机控制。其视觉质量可与大规模工业基线媲美，吞吐量提升 36 倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有世界模型在生成分钟级长视频时面临内存效率低、相机控制不精确、训练数据需求大等挑战，亟需一个高效、开源、支持精确相机控制的分钟级视频生成世界模型。

Method: (1) 混合线性注意力：结合帧级 Gated DeltaNet 和 softmax 注意力实现内存高效的长上下文建模；(2) 双分支相机控制：确保精确的 6-DoF 轨迹跟踪；(3) 两阶段生成流水线：对第一阶段输出应用长视频精炼器提升质量和一致性；(4) 鲁棒标注流水线：从公开视频中提取精确的公尺度 6-DoF 相机位姿作为动作标签。

Result: 仅用约 21.3 万公开视频片段和公尺度位姿监督，在 64 块 H100 上训练 15 天；单个 GPU 即可生成长达 60 秒的视频；蒸馏变体可在单张 RTX 5090 上通过 NVFP4 量化在 34 秒内去噪生成 60 秒 720p 视频；动作跟随准确率优于现有开源基线，视觉质量相当且吞吐量提升 36 倍。

Conclusion: SANA-WM 证明了通过精心设计的架构和标注流水线，可以在有限数据和计算资源下实现高效、高保真的分钟级世界建模，为可扩展的世界模型提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: We introduce SANA-WM, an efficient 2.6B-parameter open-source world model natively trained for one-minute generation, synthesizing high-fidelity, 720p, minute-scale videos with precise camera control. SANA-WM achieves visual quality comparable to large-scale industrial baselines such as LingBot-World and HY-WorldPlay, while significantly improving efficiency. Four core designs drive our architecture: (1) Hybrid Linear Attention combines frame-wise Gated DeltaNet (GDN) with softmax attention for memory-efficient long-context modeling. (2) Dual-Branch Camera Control ensures precise 6-DoF trajectory adherence. (3) Two-Stage Generation Pipeline applies a long-video refiner to stage-1 outputs, improving quality and consistency across sequences. (4) Robust Annotation Pipeline extracts accurate metric-scale 6-DoF camera poses from public videos to yield high-quality, spatiotemporally consistent action labels. Driven by these designs, SANA-WMdemonstrates remarkable efficiency across data, training compute, and inference hardware: it uses only $\sim$213K public video clips with metric-scale pose supervision, completes training in 15 days on 64 H100s, and generates each 60s clip on a single GPU; its distilled variant can be deployed on a single RTX 5090 with NVFP4 quantization to denoise a 60s 720p clip in 34s. On our one-minute world-model benchmark, SANA-WM demonstrates stronger action-following accuracy than prior open-source baselines and achieves comparable visual quality at $36\times$ higher throughput for scalable world modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出 SANA-WM，一个高效的 2.6B 参数开源世界模型，原生训练用于一分钟视频生成，能够合成高保真、720p、分钟级视频并实现精确的相机控制。SANA-WM 在视觉质量上可与大规模工业基线（如 LingBot-World 和 HY-WorldPlay）媲美，同时显著提升了效率。四个核心设计驱动了我们的架构：(1) 混合线性注意力（Hybrid Linear Attention）将逐帧的 Gated DeltaNet (GDN) 与 softmax 注意力相结合，实现内存高效的长上下文建模。(2) 双分支相机控制（Dual-Branch Camera Control）确保精确的 6-DoF 轨迹跟踪。(3) 两阶段生成流水线（Two-Stage Generation Pipeline）对第一阶段输出应用长视频精炼器，提升序列的质量和一致性。(4) 鲁棒标注流水线（Robust Annotation Pipeline）从公开视频中提取精确的公尺度 6-DoF 相机位姿，生成高质量、时空一致的动作标签。在这些设计的驱动下，SANA-WM 在数据、训练算力和推理硬件方面均展现出卓越的效率：仅使用约 21.3 万带有公尺度位姿监督的公开视频片段，在 64 块 H100 上 15 天完成训练，并在单个 GPU 上生成每段 60 秒视频；其蒸馏变体可部署在单张 RTX 5090 上，通过 NVFP4 量化在 34 秒内去噪生成一段 60 秒的 720p 视频。在我们的一分钟世界模型基准测试中，SANA-WM 相比现有开源基线展现出更强的动作跟随精度，在视觉质量相当的情况下吞吐量提升 36 倍，展示了可扩展的世界建模能力。

</details>


### [100] [From Plans to Pixels: Learning to Plan and Orchestrate for Open-Ended Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15181)
*Anirudh Sundara Rajan,Krishna Kumar Singh,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于经验学习的框架，通过规划器生成原子操作分解、编排器选择工具和区域执行编辑、视觉语言裁判提供奖励，实现长时序图像编辑的端到端优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型在处理抽象、多步骤指令时表现不佳，而基于智能体的方法依赖手工流程或教师模仿，缺乏灵活性与编辑结果驱动的学习。

Method: 提出经验性框架：1) 规划器生成结构化原子分解；2) 编排器选择工具和区域执行每一步；3) 视觉语言裁判基于指令遵循度和视觉质量提供奖励；4) 编排器通过最大化奖励训练，成功轨迹用于优化规划器。

Result: 该方法相比单步编辑和基于规则的多步基线，生成了更连贯、更可靠的编辑结果。

Conclusion: 将规划与奖励驱动的执行紧密耦合，能够比单步或基于规则的多步方法产生更连贯可靠的图像编辑效果。

Original Abstract: Modern image editing models produce realistic results but struggle with abstract, multi step instructions (e.g., ``make this advertisement more vegetarian-friendly''). Prior agent based methods decompose such tasks but rely on handcrafted pipelines or teacher imitation, limiting flexibility and decoupling learning from actual editing outcomes. We propose an experiential framework for long-horizon image editing, where a planner generates structured atomic decompositions and an orchestrator selects tools and regions to execute each step. A vision language judge provides outcome-based rewards for instruction adherence and visual quality. The orchestrator is trained to maximize these rewards, and successful trajectories are used to refine the planner. By tightly coupling planning with reward driven execution, our approach yields more coherent and reliable edits than single-step or rule-based multistep baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 现代图像编辑模型能够生成逼真的结果，但在处理抽象的、多步骤的指令（例如，“让这个广告更符合素食主义风格”）时表现不佳。以往基于智能体的方法虽能分解此类任务，但依赖手工构建的流程或教师模仿，限制了灵活性，并将学习与实际编辑结果脱钩。我们提出了一种用于长时序图像编辑的经验性框架：其中规划器生成结构化的原子分解，编排器选择工具和区域来执行每一步。视觉语言裁判基于指令遵循度和视觉质量提供结果驱动的奖励。编排器通过最大化这些奖励进行训练，成功轨迹被用于优化规划器。通过将规划与奖励驱动的执行紧密耦合，我们的方法相比单步或基于规则的多步基线，产生了更连贯、更可靠的编辑效果。

</details>


### [101] [Warp-as-History: Generalizable Camera-Controlled Video Generation from One Training Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15182)
*Yifan Wang,Tong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Warp-as-History接口，通过将相机畸变转换为伪历史帧输入冻结的视频生成模型，无需训练或测试时优化即可实现零样本的相机轨迹控制，仅需单视频LoRA微调即可进一步提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有相机控制视频生成方法需要后训练或测试时优化，训练成本高或推理开销大，缺乏一种简单、无需额外训练且高效的相机控制方案。

Method: Warp-as-History接口：根据目标相机轨迹，将过去观测帧进行相机畸变（warp）得到伪历史帧，对齐其位置编码与当前去噪目标帧，并移除无效源观测的畸变历史token，然后将这些伪历史帧通过模型原有的视觉历史路径输入，实现零样本相机轨迹控制。此外，可对单个相机标注视频进行轻量级LoRA微调进一步提升性能。

Result: 在多种数据集上的大量实验证实了该方法在相机轨迹跟随、视觉质量和运动动态方面的有效性，且LoRA微调后能够泛化到未见视频。

Conclusion: Warp-as-History以极简接口实现了冻结视频生成模型对相机轨迹的零样本跟随能力，无需任何训练、架构修改或测试时优化，且轻量微调可进一步提升性能。

Original Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation has made substantial progress, enabling generated videos to follow prescribed viewpoint trajectories. However, existing methods usually learn camera-specific conditioning through camera encoders, control branches, or attention and positional-encoding modifications, which often require post-training on large-scale camera-annotated videos. Training-free alternatives avoid such post-training, but often shift the cost to test-time optimization or extra denoising-time guidance. We propose Warp-as-History, a simple interface that turns camera-induced warps into camera-warped pseudo-history with target-frame positional alignment and visible-token selection. Given a target camera trajectory, we construct camera-warped pseudo-history from past observations and feed it through the model's visual-history pathway. Crucially, we align its positional encoding with the target frames being denoised and remove warped-history tokens without valid source observations. Without any training, architectural modification, or test-time optimization, this interface reveals a non-trivial zero-shot capability of a frozen video generation model to follow camera trajectories. Moreover, lightweight offline LoRA finetuning on only one camera-annotated video further improves this capability and generalizes to unseen videos, improving camera adherence, visual quality, and motion dynamics without test-time optimization or target-video adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets confirm the effectiveness of our method.

中文摘要翻译: 相机控制的视频生成已取得实质性进展，使生成的视频能够遵循指定的视角轨迹。然而，现有方法通常通过相机编码器、控制分支或注意力和位置编码修改来学习相机特定的条件，这通常需要在带相机标注的大规模视频上进行后训练。免训练替代方案避免了这种后训练，但往往将成本转移到测试时优化或额外的去噪时引导。我们提出Warp-as-History，一个简单的接口，将相机诱导的畸变(warp)转化为具有目标帧位置对齐和可见token选择的相机畸变伪历史。给定目标相机轨迹，我们从过去的观测中构建相机畸变的伪历史，并通过模型的视觉历史路径输入。关键的是，我们将其位置编码与正在去噪的目标帧对齐，并移除没有有效源观测的畸变历史token。无需任何训练、架构修改或测试时优化，该接口揭示了冻结的视频生成模型在遵循相机轨迹方面具有非平凡的零样本能力。此外，仅在单个相机标注视频上进行轻量级离线LoRA微调，即可进一步提升这种能力并泛化到未见过的视频，在没有测试时优化或目标视频适配的情况下，改善了相机遵循度、视觉质量和运动动态。在多种数据集上的大量实验证实了我们方法的有效性。

</details>


### [102] [Quantitative Video World Model Evaluation for Geometric-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15185)
*Jiaxin Wu,Yihao Pi,Yinling Zhang,Yuheng Li,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PDI-Bench，一个量化评估生成视频几何一致性的框架，通过3D重建和投影几何残差检测视频生成模型在尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性方面的失败模式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频评估方法过度依赖人工评判或学习型评分器，主观性强且难以诊断几何层面的失败（如物理上不合理的3D结构和运动），缺乏量化且可诊断的几何一致性评估工具。

Method: 对生成视频进行分割和点跟踪（SAM 2、MegaSaM、CoTracker3），通过单目重建将点提升到3D世界坐标，然后计算三类投影几何残差：尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性，并构建了包含多种压力场景的PDI-Dataset用于系统评估。

Result: 在多个最先进的视频生成器上，PDI揭示了它们存在一致的几何特定失败模式，而这些模式无法被常见的感知质量指标捕获。

Conclusion: PDI-Bench为视频生成模型的几何一致性提供了可量化的诊断信号，有助于推动生成视频向物理可信的方向发展，可作为物理世界模型的评估工具。

Original Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly studied as implicit world models, yet evaluating whether they produce physically plausible 3D structure and motion remains challenging. Most existing video evaluation pipelines rely heavily on human judgment or learned graders, which can be subjective and weakly diagnostic for geometric failures. We introduce PDI-Bench (Perspective Distortion Index), a quantitative framework for auditing geometric coherence in generated videos. Given a generated clip, we obtain object-centric observations via segmentation and point tracking (e.g., SAM 2, MegaSaM, and CoTracker3), lift them to 3D world-space coordinates via monocular reconstruction, and compute a set of projective-geometry residuals capturing three failure dimensions: scale-depth alignment, 3D motion consistency, and 3D structural rigidity. To support systematic evaluation, we build PDI-Dataset, covering diverse scenarios designed to stress these geometric constraints. Across state-of-the-art video generators, PDI reveals consistent geometry-specific failure modes that are not captured by common perceptual metrics, and provides a diagnostic signal for progress toward physically grounded video generation and physical world model. Our code and dataset can be found at https://pdi-bench.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式视频模型作为隐式世界模型的研究日益增多，但评估其能否产生物理上合理的3D结构和运动仍然具有挑战性。现有的大多数视频评估流程严重依赖人工判断或学习型评分器，这些方法具有主观性，且对几何层面的失败缺乏诊断能力。本文提出PDI-Bench（透视畸变指数），一个用于审计生成视频几何一致性的量化框架。对于给定的生成视频片段，我们通过分割和点跟踪（如SAM 2、MegaSaM和CoTracker3）获取以物体为中心的观测，通过单目重建将其提升到3D世界空间坐标，并计算一组投影几何残差，这些残差捕获了三个失败维度：尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性。为了支持系统评估，我们构建了PDI-Dataset，涵盖旨在测试这些几何约束的各种场景。在多个最先进的视频生成器上，PDI揭示了一致的几何特定失败模式，这些模式无法被常见的感知指标捕获，并为推动生成视频向物理可信的方向发展以及物理世界模型的进步提供了诊断信号。我们的代码和数据集可在https://pdi-bench.github.io/获取。

</details>


### [103] [Articraft: An Agentic System for Scalable Articulated 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15187)
*Matt Zhou,Ruining Li,Xiaoyang Lyu,Zhaomou Song,Zhening Huang,Chuanxia Zheng,Christian Rupprecht,Andrea Vedaldi,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Articraft，利用大语言模型（LLM）自动编写程序来生成大规模、高质量的关节式3D资产，并构建了包含10K+资产、覆盖245类别的数据集Articraft-10K，在资产质量和下游应用上都优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 学习理解可动关节3D物体的瓶颈在于缺乏大规模、多样化的数据集。现有数据集规模小且获取成本高，亟需一种自动化、可扩展的方式生成高质量关节式3D资产。

Method: 将生成关节式3D资产的问题转化为编写构建该资产的程序的问题。提出Articraft智能体系统：设计一个领域特定的SDK（定义部件、组合几何体、指定关节、编写测试），LLM基于此编写代码；系统提供受控的工作空间和接口，验证生成的资产并返回结构化反馈，引导LLM迭代改进。

Result: Articraft生成的资产质量优于最先进的关节式资产生成器和通用编码智能体；构建了Articraft-10K数据集（10K+资产，245类别），在关节式资产模型训练以及机器人仿真、虚拟现实等下游应用中展示了实用性。

Conclusion: 利用LLM进行程序化生成是解决关节式3D资产数据稀缺问题的有效途径；Articraft通过领域特定SDK和结构化的反馈机制，使LLM能高质量、大规模地生成关节式3D资产，为相关研究与应用提供了宝贵的数据资源。

Original Abstract: A bottleneck in learning to understand articulated 3D objects is the lack of large and diverse datasets. In this paper, we propose to leverage large language models (LLMs) to close this gap and generate articulated assets at scale. We reduce the problem of generating an articulated 3D asset to that of writing a program that builds it. We then introduce a new agentic system, Articraft, that writes such programs automatically. We design a programmatic interface and harness to help the LLM do so effectively. The LLM writes code against a domain-specific SDK for defining parts, composing geometry, specifying joints, and writing tests to validate the resulting assets. The harness exposes a restricted workspace and interface to the LLM, validates the resulting assets, and returns structured feedback. In this way, the LLM is not distracted by details such as authoring a URDF file or managing a complex software environment. We show that this produces higher-quality assets than both state-of-the-art articulated-asset generators and general-purpose coding agents. Using Articraft, we build Articraft-10K, a curated dataset of over 10K articulated assets spanning 245 categories, and show its utility both for training models of articulated assets and in downstream applications such as robotics simulation and virtual reality.

中文摘要翻译: 学习理解可动关节式3D物体的一个瓶颈在于缺乏大规模且多样化的数据集。在本文中，我们提出利用大语言模型（LLM）来弥合这一差距，并大规模生成关节式资产。我们将生成关节式3D资产的问题简化为编写构建该资产的程序。为此，我们引入了一个新的智能体系统——Articraft，它可以自动编写此类程序。我们设计了一个程序化接口和开发框架，以帮助LLM有效地完成这一任务。LLM针对一个领域特定的SDK编写代码，该SDK用于定义部件、组合几何体、指定关节以及编写测试来验证生成的资产。开发框架为LLM暴露了一个受限的工作空间和接口，验证生成的资产，并返回结构化反馈。通过这种方式，LLM不会被诸如编写URDF文件或管理复杂软件环境等细节所干扰。我们证明，该方法生成的资产质量优于最先进的关节式资产生成器和通用编程智能体。利用Articraft，我们构建了Articraft-10K，一个包含超过10K个关节式资产、涵盖245个类别的精选数据集，并展示了其在关节式资产模型训练以及机器人仿真和虚拟现实等下游应用中的实用价值。

</details>


### [104] [Aligning Latent Geometry for Spherical Flow Matching in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15193)
*Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Hidir Yesiltepe,Adil Kaan Akan,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于球面几何的潜在流匹配方法，将图像生成中的噪声和数据潜在表示投影到固定半径的球面上，并用球面线性插值替代欧几里得线性插值，使路径始终保持在球面上，从而提升生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的潜在流匹配方法沿欧几里得弦在薄球壳之间传输噪声与潜在表示，即使预处理对齐半径，弦仍会离开球壳，导致次优的生成效果。作者通过组件交换实验发现，潜在表征的语义和感知信息主要由角度（方向）承载，半径贡献很小，因此提出将生成过程约束在球面上。

Method: (1) 将每个潜在token分解为径向和角度分量；(2) 将数据潜在投影到固定token半径上；(3) 使用高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验；(4) 冻结编码器，微调解码器；(5) 用球面线性插值（SLERP）替代线性插值（LERP），使路径在每个时间步都保持在球面上，速度目标纯为角度方向。

Result: 在匹配的训练条件下，该方法在类别条件ImageNet-256上持续提升FID指标，适用于不同图像分词器，不改变扩散架构，且无需辅助编码器或表示对齐目标。

Conclusion: 将潜在流匹配约束在球面几何上是一种简单有效的方法，能提升图像生成质量，且具有较好的通用性和兼容性。

Original Abstract: Latent flow matching for image generation usually transports Gaussian noise to variational autoencoder latents along linear paths. Both endpoints, however, concentrate in thin spherical shells, and a Euclidean chord leaves those shells even when preprocessing aligns their radii. By decomposing each latent token into radial and angular components, we show through component-swap probes that decoded perceptual and semantic content is carried predominantly by direction, with radius contributing much less. We therefore project data latents onto a fixed token radius, use the radial projection of Gaussian noise as the spherical prior, finetune the decoder with the encoder frozen, and replace linear interpolation with spherical linear interpolation. The resulting geodesic paths stay on the sphere at every timestep, and their velocity targets are purely angular by construction. Under matched training, the method consistently improves class-conditional ImageNet-256 FID across different image tokenizers, leaves the diffusion architecture unchanged, and requires no auxiliary encoder or representation-alignment objective.

中文摘要翻译: 用于图像生成的潜在流匹配通常沿着线性路径将高斯噪声传输到变分自编码器的潜在表示上。然而，两个端点的分布都集中在薄球壳上，即使通过预处理对齐半径，欧几里得弦仍然会离开这些球壳。通过将每个潜在token分解为径向和角度分量，我们通过组件交换探针实验发现，解码后的感知和语义内容主要受方向决定，半径贡献很小。因此，我们将数据潜在投影到固定token半径上，使用高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验，冻结编码器并微调解码器，并用球面线性插值替代线性插值。由此产生的测地路径在每个时间步都保持在球面上，其速度目标在构造上纯为角度方向。在匹配的训练条件下，该方法在不同图像分词器上持续改善类别条件ImageNet-256的FID指标，不改变扩散架构，且无需辅助编码器或表示对齐目标。

</details>


### [105] [EntityBench: Towards Entity-Consistent Long-Range Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15199)
*Ruozhen He,Meng Wei,Ziyan Yang,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EntityBench基准（140个剧情、2,491个镜头，覆盖角色/物体/场景的跨镜头一致性评估）和EntityMem记忆增强生成系统，实验表明现有方法跨镜头一致性随距离急剧下降，而显式逐实体记忆机制在角色保真度上显著最优（Cohen's d = +2.33）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成评估通常使用独立生成的提示集，实体覆盖有限且一致性度量过于简单，难以进行标准化比较，尤其缺乏对长序列中跨镜头实体一致性的系统评估方法。

Method: 提出EntityBench基准（从真实叙事媒体提取140个剧情/2,491个镜头，含易/中/难三级难度，追踪最多50个镜头、13个跨镜头角色、8个跨镜头位置、22个跨镜头物体，最大间隔48个镜头）和EntityMem系统（在生成前将已验证的逐实体视觉参考存储到持久记忆库中）。配套三支柱评估框架：帧内质量、提示对齐、跨镜头一致性，并设置保真门控仅将准确实体外观纳入跨镜头评分。

Result: 实验表明现有方法跨镜头实体一致性随循环距离增加而急剧下降；EntityMem显式逐实体记忆机制在角色保真度上取得最高效果（Cohen's d = +2.33）和最高的角色出现率。

Conclusion: 显式逐实体记忆机制能有效提升跨镜头实体一致性，EntityBench为标准化评估提供了可靠基准，揭示了现有方法在长序列跨镜头一致性方面的严重不足。

Original Abstract: Multi-shot video generation extends single-shot generation to coherent visual narratives, yet maintaining consistent characters, objects, and locations across shots remains a challenge over long sequences. Existing evaluations typically use independently generated prompt sets with limited entity coverage and simple consistency metrics, making standardized comparison difficult. We introduce EntityBench, a benchmark of 140 episodes (2,491 shots) derived from real narrative media, with explicit per-shot entity schedules tracking characters, objects, and locations simultaneously across easy / medium / hard tiers of up to 50 shots, 13 cross-shot characters, 8 cross-shot locations, 22 cross-shot objects, and recurrence gaps spanning up to 48 shots. It is paired with a three-pillar evaluation suite that disentangles intra-shot quality, prompt-following alignment, and cross-shot consistency, with a fidelity gate that admits only accurate entity appearances into cross-shot scoring. As a baseline, we propose EntityMem, a memory-augmented generation system that stores verified per-entity visual references in a persistent memory bank before generation begins. Experiments show that cross-shot entity consistency degrades sharply with recurrence distance in existing methods, and that explicit per-entity memory yields the highest character fidelity (Cohen's d = +2.33) and presence among methods evaluated. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/.

中文摘要翻译: 多镜头视频生成将单镜头生成扩展为连贯的视觉叙事，然而在长序列中保持角色、物体和场景的一致性仍然具有挑战性。现有评估通常使用独立生成的提示集，实体覆盖有限且一致性度量过于简单，使得标准化比较变得困难。我们提出了EntityBench——一个从真实叙事媒体中提取的基准，包含140个剧情（2,491个镜头），具有显式的逐镜头实体调度，同时追踪角色、物体和场景，涵盖简单/中等/困难三个级别，最多包含50个镜头、13个跨镜头角色、8个跨镜头场景、22个跨镜头物体，循环间隔跨度达48个镜头。该基准配有一个三支柱评估套件，将帧内质量、提示遵循对齐和跨镜头一致性分离开来，并设置保真门控，仅将准确的实体外观纳入跨镜头评分。作为基线，我们提出了EntityMem——一种记忆增强生成系统，在生成开始前将已验证的逐实体视觉参考存储到持久记忆库中。实验表明，现有方法的跨镜头实体一致性随循环距离增加而急剧下降，而显式逐实体记忆在评估方法中取得了最高的角色保真度（Cohen's d = +2.33）和角色出现率。代码和数据见https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在部分可观测条件下，从视觉观测中对过去时间信号时态逻辑（ptSTL）进行认证运行时监控。提出可重复使用的监控接口，通过语义基（原子鲁棒性分数向量）实现单次校准即可认证整个公式片段，无需逐公式重新训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有运行时监控方法通常需要为每个新公式重新训练模型，效率低下。本文旨在设计一种可重复使用的监控器：一次训练和校准后，能认证目标片段内的任意公式，无需逐公式重新训练，同时提供有限样本保证。

Method: 证明语义基（原子鲁棒性分数向量）是单调、1-Lipschitz可重复使用接口类中的最小预测目标；任何公式可通过解析树派生的确定性解码器评估；单次保形校准即可认证整个片段，无需联合界。此外提出滚动预测监控器，仅预测当前谓词值并在线重建时间历史。

Result: 在行人路口基准上，滚动监控器在短时域上获得更紧的认证界，而语义基监控器在长时域上紧度提升高达4倍。在真实Waymo驾驶数据上，两种监控器均经验性地满足保形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 语义基监控器是一种理论上最优的可重复使用接口，在长时域上表现更优；滚动监控器在短时域上更紧。两种方法均可提供有限样本的认证保证，适用于实际自主系统安全监控。

Original Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了在部分可观测条件下，基于视觉观测对过去时间信号时态逻辑（ptSTL）进行认证运行时监控。监控器必须从图像中推断安全相关量并提供有限样本保证，同时具有可重复使用性：一旦训练和校准完成，它能认证目标片段中的任意公式，而无需逐公式重新训练。对于由有限时间原子字典诱导的公式片段，我们证明语义基（原子鲁棒性分数的向量）是单调、1-Lipschitz可重复使用接口类中的最小预测目标：任何公式都可通过解析树派生的确定性解码器进行评估，并且单次保形校准即可认证整个片段，无需联合界。我们还提出了一种滚动预测监控器，它仅预测当前谓词值并在线重建时间历史；该方法更易于学习，但在长时域上会变得保守。在行人路口基准测试上，滚动监控器在短时域上获得更紧的认证界，而语义基监控器在长时域上紧度提升高达4倍。我们在真实世界Waymo驾驶数据上验证了所提出的监控器，两种监控器均经验性地满足保形覆盖保证。

</details>


### [107] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAEs）从三种不同架构的EEG基础模型（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）中提取稀疏特征字典，通过临床分类法锚定特征，并引入概念引导和目标vs离靶探测指标，揭示模型内部表征的临床可解释性问题，包括"破坏球"干预和年龄-病理混杂等关键缺陷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务上表现优异，但其内部计算过程不透明，阻碍了临床信任的建立。需要一种方法打开这些模型的"黑箱"，理解其内部表征与临床概念之间的对应关系。

Method: 1) 使用TopK稀疏自编码器从三种不同架构（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）的EEG transformer嵌入中提取稀疏特征字典；2) 将特征锚定到临床分类法（异常、年龄、性别、用药）上，基准测试单语义性和纠缠度；3) 引入单一超参数流程，通过内在字典健康审计跨架构迁移；4) 通过概念引导，提出"目标vs离靶"探测区域指标量化引导选择性；5) 使用频谱解码器将潜在操作映射回幅度频谱。

Result: 1) 单一超参数流程可稳健跨三种架构迁移；2) 揭示了三种操作机制：可选择引导、已编码但纠缠、未编码；3) 发现关键表征失败模式："破坏球"干预会破坏全局模型性能，以及临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混杂）导致无法独立操控单一概念；4) 频谱解码器可将潜在操作转化为生理上可解释的频率特征（如病理慢波抑制和α频带恢复）。

Conclusion: 该框架系统性地暴露了EEG基础模型在临床可解释性方面的关键缺陷，包括表征纠缠和干预破坏性问题，并提供了一种从频域角度理解模型内部操作的生理可解释途径，为临床信任的建立提供了分析工具。

Original Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

中文摘要翻译: EEG基础模型在临床性能上达到了最先进水平，但其驱动预测的内部计算仍然不透明——这是临床信任的一大障碍。我们应用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAEs）跨越三种架构不同的EEG变换器模型——SleepFM、REVE和LaBraM——从其嵌入中提取稀疏特征字典。通过将这些特征锚定到临床分类法（异常、年龄、性别和用药）上，我们基准测试了跨架构的单语义性和纠缠度。一个由内在字典健康审计驱动的单一超参数流程，可稳健地迁移到全部三种架构。通过概念引导，我们引入了一个"目标vs离靶"探测区域指标来量化引导选择性，并揭示了三种操作机制：可选择引导、已编码但纠缠、以及未编码。该框架暴露了关键的表征失败模式："破坏球"干预会破坏全局模型性能，以及临床纠缠（例如年龄-病理混杂），其中抑制一个概念不可避免地破坏另一个概念。最后，一个频谱解码器将这些干预映射回幅度频谱，将潜在操作转化为生理上可解释的频率特征，例如病理慢波抑制和α频带恢复。

</details>


### [108] [REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867)
*Peicheng Wu,Zhenyu Bu,Runze Ma,Lin Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REALM框架，通过回顾式蒸馏将非因果的LFP解码模型转化为因果模型，实现实时神经解码，性能优于现有方法且参数量减半、训练时间缩短10倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尖峰信号高采样率导致高功耗和带宽需求，成为高通道数无线BCI的瓶颈；LFP虽具有长期稳定性和低功耗优势，但基于LFP的解码模型准确率较低且通常采用非因果架构，无法实时部署。

Method: 首先预训练一个双向Mamba-2教师模型（掩码自编码目标），然后通过表示对齐与任务监督的联合目标，将教师模型蒸馏为一个紧凑的因果学生模型，实现离线到在线的知识迁移。

Result: REALM在行为解码上一致优于基于LFP的因果和非因果SOTA方法；参数量减少2倍，训练时间减少10倍，且无需依赖尖峰信号即可达到有竞争力的解码性能。

Conclusion: 回顾式蒸馏有效弥合了离线与实时神经解码之间的差距，证明仅使用LFP的模型可替代尖峰信号实现高性能解码，为下一代无线植入式BCI提供了实用且可扩展的方案。

Original Abstract: Spike activity has been the dominant neural signal for behavior decoding due to its high spatial and temporal resolution. However, as brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) move toward high channel counts and wireless operation, the high sampling frequency of spike signals becomes a bottleneck due to high power and bandwidth requirements. Local field potentials (LFPs) represent a different spatial-temporal scale of brain activity compared to spikes, offering key advantages including improved long-term stability, reduced energy consumption, and lower bandwidth requirement. Despite these benefits, LFP-based decoding models typically show reduced accuracy and often rely on non-causal architectures that are unsuitable for real-time deployment. To address these challenges, we propose REALM: a retrospective distillation framework that enables causal LFP decoding. Inspired by offline-to-online distillation strategies in speech recognition, REALM transfers representational knowledge from a pretrained multi-session bidirectional LFP model to a causal version for real-time deployment. We first pretrain a bidirectional Mamba-2 teacher model using a masked autoencoding objective. We then distill this teacher model into a compact student model via a combined objective of representation alignment and task supervision. REALM consistently outperforms both causal and non-causal LFP-based SOTA methods for behavior decoding. Notably, our REALM improves decoding performance while achieving a $2\times$ reduction in parameter count and a $10\times$ reduction in training time. These results demonstrate that retrospective distillation effectively bridges the gap between offline and real-time neural decoding. REALM shows that LFP-only models can achieve competitive decoding performance without reliance on spike signals, offering a practical and scalable alternative for next-generation wireless implantable BCIs.

中文摘要翻译: 由于尖峰信号具有高空间和时间分辨率，它一直是行为解码中占主导地位的神经信号。然而，随着脑机接口（BCI）向高通道数和无线操作发展，尖峰信号的高采样频率因高功耗和高带宽需求而成为瓶颈。与尖峰信号相比，局部场电位（LFP）代表了不同时空尺度的脑活动，具有长期稳定性更好、能耗更低、带宽需求更小等关键优势。尽管有这些优点，基于LFP的解码模型通常准确率较低，且往往依赖不适合实时部署的非因果架构。为应对这些挑战，我们提出REALM：一种能够实现因果LFP解码的回顾式蒸馏框架。受语音识别中离线到在线蒸馏策略的启发，REALM将预训练的多会话双向LFP模型中的表征知识迁移到因果版本中，以支持实时部署。我们首先使用掩码自编码目标预训练一个双向Mamba-2教师模型，然后通过表示对齐与任务监督的联合目标将该教师模型蒸馏为一个紧凑的学生模型。REALM在行为解码上持续优于基于LFP的因果和非因果最先进方法。值得注意的是，REALM在提升解码性能的同时，实现了参数量减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍。这些结果表明，回顾式蒸馏有效弥合了离线与实时神经解码之间的差距。REALM证明，仅使用LFP的模型无需依赖尖峰信号即可达到有竞争力的解码性能，为下一代无线植入式BCI提供了一种实用且可扩展的替代方案。

</details>


### [109] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Joint-VAE框架，通过架构退火（architectural annealing）替代手动调参，在混合连续-离散潜空间中分离dMRI的采集效应与生物变异，无需监督信号即可恢复扫描仪/协议相关的聚类结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: dMRI数据中不同站点、扫描仪和协议带来的采集差异会引入变异性，干扰结构连接组分析。传统降维方法将所有变异视为连续变量，采集效应易被吸收进连续潜空间；而现有的混合潜空间模型虽引入离散分量，但需手动调节容量以捕获预期变异，缺乏自动化机制。

Method: 提出无监督框架Joint-VAE，在解码器前对编码器输出进行**架构退火（architectural annealing）**，使模型在训练过程中自适应平衡离散与连续潜变量，无需手动容量调参。构建了N=7,416个结构连接组数据集（年龄2-102岁，13项研究，25种采集参数组合）。对比标准VAE、PCA+k-means聚类以及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型。

Result: 架构退火方法在站点学习上显著优于基线（ARI=0.53, p<0.05）。Joint-VAE通过联合建模平滑与类别结构，能够恢复与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类。

Conclusion: 混合连续-离散潜空间结合架构退火（而非损失退火），为捕获dMRI中的采集变异性提供了一种有效的无监督机制，无需手动调参即可分离采集效应与生物变异。

Original Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

中文摘要翻译: 不同站点、扫描仪和协议下的扩散磁共振成像（dMRI）采集差异会引入变异性，使结构连接组分析复杂化。这促使人们开发深度学习模型，能够在低维空间中表示高维连接组，同时明确地将采集相关效应与生物变异分离开来。传统降维方法将所有变异建模为连续变量，因此采集效应常常被吸收进连续潜空间。近年来的混合潜空间模型通过结合离散和连续分量来应对这一问题，但通常需要手动调节容量以确保离散分量捕获到预期变异。我们提出了一种无监督框架，通过在解码前对编码器输出进行**架构退火（architectural annealing）**来消除这一手动调参过程，使模型能够在训练期间自适应地平衡离散和连续潜变量。为评估该框架，我们构建了一个包含N=7,416个来自dMRI的结构连接组数据集，覆盖年龄2至102岁、13项研究中的25种独特采集参数组合。其中5,900例为认知未受损，877例为轻度认知障碍（MCI），639例为阿尔茨海默病（AD）。我们将其与标准VAE、PCA结合k-means聚类以及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型进行对比。结果表明，我们的架构退火在站点学习上优于上述基线（调整兰德指数ARI=0.53, p<0.05）。实验结果显示，采用架构退火（而非损失退火）的混合连续-离散潜空间，为捕获dMRI中的采集变异性提供了一种有用的无监督机制：通过联合建模平滑结构和类别结构，Joint-VAE能够恢复与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类。

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Mode-Seeking RL: Trajectory-Balance Post-Training for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13935)
*Saba Ahmadi,Prasanna Parthasarathi,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TraFL方法通过轨迹平衡目标解决扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题，在数学推理和代码生成任务上优于基线和现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型的后训练方法大多采用奖励最大化目标，导致"轨迹锁定"问题：采样驱动的奖励更新将概率质量过度集中在狭窄的去噪路径上，降低了重复采样时覆盖其他正确解的能力。

Method: 提出TraFL（轨迹流平衡）方法：采用轨迹平衡目标，将策略训练向一个以冻结参考模型为锚点的、经奖励倾斜的目标分布；通过扩散兼容的序列级替代目标和学习到的与提示相关的归一化项使其适用于扩散语言模型。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，TraFL是唯一在所有基准长度设定下均优于基础模型的评估后训练方法，且优势随采样预算增加而持续。在Minerva Math上保持优于基础模型，在LiveCodeBench的所有难度划分上均表现最强。

Conclusion: TraFL有效解决了扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题，在多种设定下稳定提升模型性能，且泛化能力良好。

Original Abstract: Diffusion language models are a promising alternative to autoregressive models, yet post-training methods for them largely adapt reward-maximizing objectives. We identify a central failure mode in this setting we call trajectory locking: sampled reward-driven updates over-concentrate probability mass onto a narrow set of denoising paths, reducing coverage of alternative correct solutions under repeated sampling. To address this, we propose TraFL (Trajectory Flow baLancing), a trajectory-balance objective that trains the policy toward a reward-tilted target distribution anchored to a frozen reference model. We make this practical for diffusion language models with a diffusion-compatible sequence-level surrogate and a learned prompt-dependent normalization. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, TraFL is the only evaluated post-training method that improves over the base model in every benchmark-length setting, with gains that persist as the sampling budget increases. The improvements transfer to held-out evaluations: TraFL stays above the base model on Minerva Math and is the strongest method on every LiveCodeBench difficulty split.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散语言模型是自回归模型的一种有前景的替代方案，然而针对它们的后训练方法在很大程度上采用了奖励最大化目标。我们发现这种设定中存在一个核心失败模式，我们称之为轨迹锁定：采样驱动的奖励更新将概率质量过度集中到一组狭窄的去噪路径上，从而在重复采样时降低了对其他正确解的覆盖。为解决这一问题，我们提出了TraFL（轨迹流平衡），一种轨迹平衡目标，它将策略训练导向一个以冻结的参考模型为锚点的、经奖励倾斜的目标分布。我们通过一个扩散兼容的序列级替代目标和一个学习到的与提示相关的归一化项，使其适用于扩散语言模型。在数学推理和代码生成基准上，TraFL是唯一在所有基准长度设定下均优于基础模型的评估后训练方法，并且其优势随着采样预算的增加而持续存在。这些改进还迁移到了留出评估上：TraFL在Minerva Math上保持优于基础模型，并且在LiveCodeBench的每个难度划分上都是最强的方法。

</details>


### [111] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem提出了一种自我进化的记忆架构，不仅存储内容会演化，检索机制（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略）也会通过LLM驱动的诊断模块自动调整，在LoCoMo和MemBench上分别超越最强基线25.7%和18.9%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的记忆系统将检索基础设施视为固定的——存储内容可以更新，但评分函数、融合策略和答案生成策略在部署后保持不变。作者认为真正的自适应记忆需要在存储知识和检索机制两个层面共同演化。

Method: EvolveMem是一种自我进化的记忆架构，将完整的检索配置暴露为结构化动作空间，由一个LLM驱动的诊断模块优化。每个演化轮次中，该模块读取每个问题的失败日志、识别根因并提出针对性配置调整；守卫元分析器在应用调整时带有自动回退（回归时回滚）和停滞时探索的保护机制，构成闭环自我演化的AutoResearch过程。

Result: 在LoCoMo上，EvolveMem超越最强基线25.7%（相对），相比最弱基线提升78.0%；在MemBench上超越最强基线18.9%（相对）。演化后的配置可在不同基准间正迁移，说明自我演化过程捕获了通用检索原理而非数据集特定启发式规则。

Conclusion: 自我演化的记忆架构能够自动发现有效的检索策略（包括原始动作空间中不存在的全新配置维度），实现从人工调参到自主架构研究的转变，且学到的策略具有跨基准的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

中文摘要翻译: 长时记忆对于跨多个会话操作的LLM智能体至关重要，然而现有记忆系统将检索基础设施视为固定的：存储内容可以演化，但评分函数、融合策略和答案生成策略在部署后保持不变。我们认为，真正自适应的记忆需要在两个层面共同演化：存储的知识以及查询它的检索机制。我们提出了EvolveMem，一种自我演化的记忆架构，它将完整的检索配置暴露为一个结构化的动作空间，由一个LLM驱动的诊断模块进行优化。在每个演化轮次中，该模块读取每个问题的失败日志，识别根因，并提出针对性的配置调整；守卫元分析器在应用调整时带有自动回退（回归时回滚）和停滞时探索的保护机制。这种闭环的自我演化实现了一个AutoResearch过程：系统自主地对其自身架构进行迭代研究循环，取代了人工配置调优。从一个最简基线出发，该过程自主收敛，发现了有效的检索策略，包括原始动作空间中不存在的全新配置维度。在LoCoMo上，EvolveMem超越最强基线25.7%（相对），相比最弱基线实现了78.0%的相对提升。在MemBench上，EvolveMem超越最强基线18.9%（相对）。演化后的配置可在不同基准之间实现正向迁移而非灾难性迁移，表明自我演化过程捕获的是通用检索原理而非基准特定的启发式规则。代码已开源在 https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem。

</details>


### [112] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将加权InfoNCE目标函数解释为距离几何问题，揭示了对比学习中表征几何的精确特性，分析了不同加权方案下最优嵌入的结构，为设计对比学习目标提供了理论框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对比学习旨在通过表征学习保留样本间的相似性图结构，但所得嵌入的几何特性尚不明确，缺乏对最优嵌入几何结构的精确刻画。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标函数重新解释为距离几何问题，通过理论分析精确刻画了多种监督和弱监督对比学习目标下最优嵌入的几何结构。

Result: (1) 监督分类中SupCon和Soft SupCon都将类内样本坍缩为单原型；平衡SupCon恢复经典正则单纯形几何，而类别不平衡时SupCon产生非均匀类间相似度，Soft SupCon则保持正则单纯形。(2) 连续标签场景下，y-Aware CL在标签不位于超球面时无法达到熵最优，而几何一致的加权方案（如欧几里得-欧几里得加权或X-CLR）具有唯一最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 加权方案决定了对比学习是否几何可实现、退化或不一致，为设计对比学习目标提供了原则性框架。

Original Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

中文摘要翻译: 对比学习旨在通过学习反映相似性图的表征来保留样本之间的关系结构。然而，所得嵌入的几何特性仍鲜为人知。本文证明，加权InfoNCE目标函数可以被解释为距离几何问题，其中加权方案指定了表征需要实现的目标几何结构。这一视角为多种监督和弱监督目标函数下的最优嵌入提供了精确刻画。在监督分类中，SupCon和Soft SupCon（其密集松弛版本，不同类别的样本对具有较小的非零相似度）都将每个类别内的样本坍缩为单一原型。然而，平衡的SupCon恢复了经典的正则单纯形几何，而类别不平衡破坏了这种对称性：SupCon根据类别大小产生非均匀的类间相似度，而Soft SupCon则无论类别不平衡如何都保持正则单纯形几何。在连续标签场景中，我们的框架揭示了另一种失效模式：y-Aware对比学习通常无法达到其熵最优状态，除非标签位于超球面上，这暴露了欧几里得标签权重与球形潜在相似性之间的不匹配。相比之下，几何一致的加权方案（如欧几里得-欧几里得加权或X-CLR）具有唯一的最优嵌入。我们的结果表明，加权方案的选择决定了对比学习是几何可实现的、退化的还是不一致的，为设计对比学习目标提供了原则性框架。

</details>


### [113] [Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13950)
*Darius A. Faroughy,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Siddharth Mishra-Sharma,David Shih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Collider-Bench是一个评估LLM智能体在长期工具使用任务中再现大型强子对撞机（LHC）实验分析能力的基准。智能体需基于公开论文和科学软件，将分析管道转化为可执行的模拟-筛选流程，并提交预测结果。评估采用连续直方图指标和LLM法官检测失败模式。实验表明，尚无通用编码智能体可靠超越物理学家参与的解决方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准难以捕捉真实科学工作的复杂性和细微差别，尤其是大型强子对撞机实验分析的再现性挑战。由于公开工具链仅近似内部软件，且论文省略实现细节，需要智能体具备物理推理和试错能力来填补信息空白。

Method: 构建Collider-Bench基准，包含基于LHC搜索的分析任务；智能体需将已发表分析转化为可执行的模拟-筛选管道，并提交指定信号区域中的预测碰撞事件产量；使用标准直方图指标计算连续保真度分数；通过LLM法官评估代码和会话轨迹以检测伪造、幻觉和重复等故障模式；提供容器化沙箱和事件模拟工具。

Result: 在通用编码智能体的能力阶梯上进行了评估，结果显示平均而言，没有任何智能体能够可靠地击败物理学家参与的解决方案。

Conclusion: 当前通用编码智能体在复杂科学工作流再现任务上的表现仍不及有物理学家参与的半自动方案，凸显了科学领域专用智能体设计的必要性。

Original Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tool-use tasks, but existing benchmarks rarely capture the complexity and nuance of real scientific work. To address this gap, we introduce Collider-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can reproduce experimental analyses from the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) using only public papers and open scientific software. Such analyses are often difficult to reproduce because the public toolchain only approximates the software used internally by the experimental collaborations, while the published papers inevitably omit implementation details needed for a faithful reconstruction. Agents must therefore rely on physical reasoning, domain knowledge, and trial-and-error to fill these gaps. Each task requires the agent to turn a published analysis into an executable simulation-and-selection pipeline and submit predicted collision event yields in specified signal regions. These predictions are evaluated with standard histogram metrics that provide continuous fidelity scores without a hand-written rubric. We also report the computational cost incurred by each agent per task. Finally, we evaluate the codebase and full session trace using an LLM judge to catch qualitative failure modes such as fabrications, hallucinations and duplications. We release an initial set of tasks drawn from LHC searches, together with a containerized sandbox and event simulation tools. We evaluate across a capability ladder of general purpose coding agents. Our results show that on average no agent reliably beats the physicist-in-the-loop solution.

中文摘要翻译: 自主语言模型智能体越来越多地通过长周期工具使用任务进行评估，但现有基准很少能捕捉真实科学工作的复杂性和细微差别。为填补这一空白，我们引入了Collider-Bench——一个评估LLM智能体能否仅使用公开论文和开源科学软件再现大型强子对撞机（LHC）实验分析的基准。这类分析往往难以复现，因为公开工具链仅能近似实验合作组内部使用的软件，而发表论文又不可避免地遗漏了忠实重建所需的实现细节。因此，智能体必须依赖物理推理、领域知识和试错来填补这些空白。每个任务要求智能体将已发表分析转化为可执行的模拟与筛选管道，并在指定信号区域提交预测的碰撞事件产量。这些预测使用标准直方图指标进行评估，无需人工编写评分细则即可提供连续保真度分数。我们还报告了每个智能体每项任务的计算成本。最后，我们使用LLM法官评估代码和完整会话轨迹，以捕获伪造、幻觉和重复等定性失败模式。我们发布了一组源自LHC搜索的初始任务，并附带容器化沙箱和事件模拟工具。我们在通用编码智能体的能力阶梯上进行了评估。结果表明，平均而言，没有任何智能体能够可靠地击败物理学家参与的解决方案。

</details>


### [114] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过将标准高斯源分布替换为基于历史动作构建的WarmPrior，在机器人操作任务中持续提升成功率，其效果源于更直的路径概率，并改善了先验空间强化学习的探索分布。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略使用标准高斯分布作为源分布，但这一选择可能并非最优，源分布作为生成式机器人控制中的设计维度尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出WarmPrior方法，利用可获得的近期动作历史构建一个简单的时间锚定先验分布，替代标准高斯源分布，用于生成式机器人控制策略。

Result: 1) 在机器人操作任务上持续提升成功率；2) 生成更直的概率路径，与Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果类似；3) 在先验空间强化学习中改善探索分布，提升样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但尚未充分探索的设计维度，WarmPrior通过简单替换源分布即可带来显著性能提升。

Original Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略已成为视觉运动机器人控制的主导范式。我们发现，将标准高斯源分布替换为WarmPrior（一种基于近期可获得的动作历史构建的简单时间锚定先验）能够持续提升机器人操作任务的成功率。我们将这一收益归因于明显更直的概率路径，这呼应了Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果。除了标准行为克隆外，WarmPrior还重塑了先验空间强化学习中的探索分布，同时提升了样本效率和最终性能。综合这些结果，识别出源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要且尚未充分探索的设计维度。

</details>


### [115] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个全面的能耗核算框架，用于测量知识蒸馏和合成数据微调两种蒸馏方法的完整端到端能耗与排放，揭示了以往被忽略的教师侧成本，并给出了能耗约束下的设计规则与开源测量工具。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有关于蒸馏能效的主张通常未考虑完整的端到端能耗，包括教师侧的数据生成、logit缓存和评估等关键环节，导致对能耗和资源成本的评估不全面。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立可量化的能耗核算体系。

Method: 提出一个分阶段追踪GPU设备功耗的全面能耗核算框架，通过实验分离并记录不同阶段的能耗，系统测量了基于logit的传统知识蒸馏和基于合成数据的监督微调两种常见蒸馏方法的能耗与排放，构建了能耗-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 通过实证测量和分析，揭示了蒸馏过程中被忽略的教师侧成本，构建了能耗-质量帕累托前沿，并得出了在能耗和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则。

Conclusion: 发布了开源测量工具与核算协议，为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供了标准化基础，使完整pipeline的能耗影响得到明确核算。

Original Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型的广泛部署推动了对GPU需求和数据中心规模的激增，引发了关于电力消耗、电网压力以及现代AI工作负载影响的担忧。蒸馏通常被认为是获得更便宜、更高效模型的最有效途径之一，但这些主张很少考虑完整的端到端能耗与资源成本，包括关键的教师侧工作负载，如数据生成、logit缓存和评估。我们提出了一个全面的能耗核算框架，通过详细的阶段级GPU设备功耗追踪，测量蒸馏管线的完整计算成本。在我们的实验中，我们跨不同阶段分离并记录了经验能耗，系统测量了两种常见蒸馏方法——基于logit的传统知识蒸馏和基于合成数据的监督微调——的能耗与排放，构建了揭示先前被忽略成本的能耗-质量帕累托前沿。通过这些测量和分析，我们推导出在能耗和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则，并发布了一个开源测量工具与核算协议，为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供标准化基础，明确核算完整管线的能耗影响。

</details>


### [116] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3 是一个面向表格数据的预训练基础模型，通过纯合成数据预训练和测试时计算缩放，在百万行级数据集上实现了显著优于现有方法的速度与性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 表格数据在科学与工业中占据核心地位，现有模型在处理大规模数据集（百万行级）时性能或效率不足，用户反馈也表明需要更快的训练/推理速度和更强的跨模态（时间序列、关系数据、表格文本）泛化能力。

Method: 基于先验知识在纯合成数据上预训练，引入测试时计算缩放（test-time compute scaling），通过减少 KV 缓存和行分块（row-chunking）技术实现单卡 H100 上百万行数据的快速推理。

Result: 在 TabArena 上显著超越所有基线模型（包括调参集成模型）；在百万行级、200 特征数据集上击败 8 小时调参的 GBDT 基线；TabPFN-3-Plus 在 TabArena 上领先非 TabPFN 模型 200+ Elo；在关系数据（RelBenchV1）、表格文本数据（TabSTAR）和时间序列（fev-bench）上均达到或接近 SOTA；速度上比 TabPFN-2.5 快 20 倍，比 AutoGluon 1.5 extreme 快 10 倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3 通过合成数据预训练和测试时计算缩放，在表格预测领域实现了新的性能-速度帕累托前沿，并成功将基础模型能力扩展到关系数据、时间序列和表格文本等多样化的数据模态。

Original Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

中文摘要翻译: 表格数据支撑着科学和工业中大多数高价值预测问题，TabPFN 推动了该模态的基础模型革命。基于用户反馈，TabPFN-3 在此基础上扩展了最先进性能，使其能够处理多达 100 万条训练行的数据集，并大幅减少了训练和推理时间。TabPFN-3 完全基于我们先验知识生成的合成数据进行预训练，极大地推进了表格预测的前沿，并在时间序列、关系数据和表格文本数据上带来了显著提升。在标准表格基准 TabArena 上，TabPFN-3 的一次前向传播以显著优势超越了所有其他模型（包括调参和集成的基线），并在速度/性能的帕累托前沿上占据主导地位。在更多样化的数据集上，TabPFN-3 在多类别数据集中排名第一，并在多达 100 万训练行和 200 个特征的数据集上击败了经过 8 小时调参的梯度提升树基线。TabPFN-3 将测试时计算缩放引入表格基础模型。我们的 API 产品 TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) 利用这一特性，在 TabArena 上以超过 200 Elo 的优势击败所有非 TabPFN 模型，在最大数据子集上 Elo 优势升至 420，并且在性能上优于 AutoGluon 1.5 extreme 的同时速度快 10 倍，且无需使用 LLM、真实数据、互联网搜索或除 TabPFN 之外的任何其他模型。TabPFN-3 扩展了我们模型的能力，实现了对关系数据（在 RelBenchV1 上达到新的 SOTA 基础模型）和表格文本数据（通过 TabPFN-3-Plus 在 TabSTAR 上达到 SOTA）的最先进预测；并改进了现有集成：专用检查点 TabPFN-TS-3 在时间序列基准 fev-bench 上排名第二，且 SHAP 值计算速度最高提升 120 倍。TabPFN-3 在实现这一性能的同时，速度比 TabPFN-2.5 快 20 倍。此外，通过减少 KV 缓存和行分块技术，可在单张 H100 上扩展到 100 万行数据并实现快速推理速度。

</details>


### [117] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了离散扩散模型（包括均匀扩散和吸收/掩码扩散）的去噪过程存在层次结构：在最终去噪步骤的小噪声阶段，支持结构（如语法有效性）先于频率信息被学习。均匀扩散中编辑分为三类（改善、保持、恶化有效性），而吸收扩散的主导项集中在改善有效性的移动上。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管离散扩散模型在语言建模中越来越有竞争力，但其去噪目标如何组织学习过程尚不清楚，特别是模型如何区分支持结构（如语法有效性）与细粒度的频率信息。

Method: 通过理论分析证明均匀扩散和吸收扩散在最终去噪步骤中的反向编辑分解为领先尺度（决定是否向数据支持移动）和精细系数（决定同一尺度内的相对概率），并通过实验验证了支持结构先于频率排名被学习。

Result: 1) 支持局部化（validity结构）比支持内频率排名更早出现；2) 均匀扩散与吸收扩散之间的对比符合预测的速率分离；3) 均匀扩散呈现三分法（改善、保持、恶化有效性的编辑），吸收扩散的主导质量集中在改善有效性的移动上。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型先学习数据支持（如语法有效性结构），后学习数据频率信息，且不同扩散机制的分离模式不同。

Original Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.
